73         ENDIF.

280      CASE old_ok_code.

282        WHEN ''.

288    *        ************************* START OF CHNGES : BY BIPIN SHUKLA : TO HIDE FROM REQUEST CREATION
289            IF screen-name = 'GRC_RISK'. "AND SY-TCODE EQ 'ZIC_AUTH_FI'.
290              screen-invisible = 1.
291    *         SCREEN-INPUT = 1.
292              MODIFY SCREEN.
293            ENDIF.
294
295            IF  screen-name = 'SEC_LEVEL'. "AND SY-TCODE EQ 'ZIC_AUTH_FI' .
296              screen-invisible = 1.
297    *         SCREEN-INPUT = 0.
298              MODIFY SCREEN.
299            ENDIF.
300
301            IF  screen-name = 'GICON' ."AND SY-TCODE EQ 'ZIC_AUTH_FI' .
302              screen-invisible = 1.
303    *         SCREEN-INPUT = 0.
304              MODIFY SCREEN.
305            ENDIF.
306            IF  screen-name = 'GRC_RAL'. " AND SY-TCODE EQ 'ZIC_AUTH_FI' .
307              screen-active = 0.
308              MODIFY SCREEN.
309            ENDIF.
310            IF  screen-name = 'GRC_RPL'. "AND SY-TCODE EQ 'ZIC_AUTH_FI' .
311              screen-active = 0.
312              MODIFY SCREEN.
313            ENDIF.
314            IF  screen-name = 'RISK_DESC'." AND SY-TCODE EQ 'ZIC_AUTH_FI' .
315              screen-active = 0.
316              MODIFY SCREEN.
317            ENDIF.
318
319    ************************* END OF CHNGES : BY BIPIN SHUKLA : TO HIDE FROM REQUEST CREATION
320
321          ENDLOOP.
322
323        WHEN 'CREATE' OR 'ROLE_DEL'.

933    *&spwizard: output module for tc 'TABCTRL100'. do not change this line!
934    *&spwizard: move itab to dynpro
935    MODULE tabctrl100_move OUTPUT.
936      MOVE-CORRESPONDING g_tabctrl100_wa TO zic_prep_rolerei.
937      IF NOT zic_prep_rolerei-role_name IS INITIAL.
938        zic_prep_rolerei-docno = zic_prep_rolereq-docno.
939
940        IF old_ok_code = 'CRCROLES' OR zic_prep_rolereq-crc_fl = 'X'.
941          SELECT * FROM ZMM_PREP_ROLECRC UP TO 1 ROWS
942     WHERE ROLE_TYPE =
943     ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-ROLE_NAME
944     ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
945     ENDSELECT.
946          IF sy-subrc = 0 .
947            MOVE zmm_prep_rolecrc-brief_desc TO role_desc.
948          ENDIF.
949        ELSE.
950          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_prep_roledes WHERE role_type =
951                      zic_prep_rolerei-role_name.
952          IF sy-subrc = 0 .
953            MOVE zmm_prep_roledes-brief_desc TO role_desc.
954          ENDIF.
955        ENDIF.
956
957      ENDIF.
958    ENDMODULE.                    "TABCTRL100_move OUTPUT

997    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
998    *&      Module  TABCTRL100_attrib  OUTPUT
999    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1000   *       text
1001   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1002   MODULE tabctrl100_attrib OUTPUT.
1003
1004     IF old_ok_code <> 'DISPLAY' AND old_ok_code <> ''.
1005
1006       IF old_ok_code <> 'CRCROLES'.
1007         IF old_ok_code = 'CREATE'.
1008         ELSEIF zic_prep_rolereq-crc_fl = 'X'.
1009           crc_check_fl = 'X'.
1010         ENDIF.
1011       ELSE.
1012         crc_check_fl = 'X'.
1013       ENDIF.
1014
1015       IF crc_check_fl <> 'X' .
1016
1017         CLEAR crc_check_fl.
1018
1019         SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_prep_roledes WHERE role_type =
1020                                                   g_tabctrl100_wa-role_name.
1021

1454   *&      Module  set_title  OUTPUT
1455   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1456   *       text
1457   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1458   MODULE set_title OUTPUT.
1459
1460     IF zic_prep_rolereq-crossco_fl = 'X'.
1461       g_text = ' : Cross Company Authorisation'.
1462       SET TITLEBAR 'PREP_TITLE' WITH g_text.
1463       SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1464     ENDIF.
1465     IF old_ok_code = 'CREATE' AND ( okcode_100 = '' OR
1466         okcode_100 = 'CREATE' ) .
1467       MOVE 'ATTACH' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1468       APPEND wa_tab TO it_tab.
1469       MOVE 'LIST' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1470       APPEND wa_tab TO it_tab.
1471       SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1472     ENDIF.
1473     IF old_ok_code = 'CHANGE' AND ( okcode_100 = '' OR
1474         okcode_100 = 'CHANGE' OR okcode_100 = 'LIST' ) .
1475       IF zic_prep_rolereq-crc_fl = 'X' OR
1476          zic_prep_rolereq-crossco_fl = 'X'.
1477       ELSE.
1478         MOVE 'ATTACH' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1479         APPEND wa_tab TO it_tab.
1480       ENDIF.
1481       SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1482     ENDIF.
1483
1484     IF old_ok_code = 'DELETE' AND ( okcode_100 = '' OR
1485         okcode_100 = 'DELETE' OR okcode_100 = 'LIST' ) .
1486       MOVE 'ATTACH' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1487       APPEND wa_tab TO it_tab.
1488       SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1489     ENDIF.
1490
1491     IF old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY'
1492        AND zic_prep_rolereq-comm_fl = 'X'.
1493       SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1494     ELSE.
1495
1496       IF old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY' AND ( okcode_100 = '' OR
1497           okcode_100 = 'DISPLAY' OR okcode_100 = 'LIST' ) .
1498         MOVE 'ATTACH' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1499         APPEND wa_tab TO it_tab.
1500         SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1501       ENDIF.
1502
1503     ENDIF.
1504
1505     IF old_ok_code = 'APPROVE' AND ( okcode_100 = '' OR
1506         okcode_100 = 'APPROVE' OR okcode_100 = 'LIST' ) .
1507       MOVE 'ATTACH' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1508       APPEND wa_tab TO it_tab.
1509       SET PF-STATUS 'OPTNS' EXCLUDING it_tab.
1510     ENDIF.
1511
1512     IF zic_prep_rolereq-crc_fl = 'X'.
1513       g_text = ' : CRC Authorisation'.
1514       SET TITLEBAR 'PREP_TITLE' WITH g_text.
1515     ENDIF.

1562   *&spwizard: output module for tc 'TABLCTRL110'. do not change this line!
1563   *&spwizard: move itab to dynpro
1564   MODULE tablctrl110_move OUTPUT.
1565
1566     MOVE-CORRESPONDING g_tablctrl110_wa TO zic_prep_rolerei.
1567
1568     IF NOT zic_prep_rolerei-role_name IS INITIAL.
1569       zic_prep_rolerei-docno = zic_prep_rolereq-docno.
1570
1571       IF old_ok_code = 'CRCROLES' OR zic_prep_rolereq-crc_fl = 'X'.
1572         SELECT * FROM ZMM_PREP_ROLECRC UP TO 1 ROWS
1573    WHERE ROLE_TYPE =
1574    ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-ROLE_NAME
1575    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1576    ENDSELECT.
1577         IF sy-subrc = 0 .
1578           MOVE zmm_prep_rolecrc-brief_desc TO role_desc.
1579         ENDIF.
1580         SELECT * FROM ZMM_PREP_CRCDESG UP TO 1 ROWS
1581    WHERE ROLE_TYPE =
1582    ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-ROLE_NAME AND ROLE_TYPE_EX = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-ROLE_TYPE_EX
1583    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1584    ENDSELECT.
1585         IF sy-subrc = 0 AND zic_prep_rolerei-plant <> ''.
1586           MOVE zmm_prep_crcdesg-crc_pos TO crc_pos.
1587         ENDIF.
1588       ELSE.
1589         SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_prep_roledes WHERE role_type =
1590                     zic_prep_rolerei-role_name.
1591         IF sy-subrc = 0 .
1592           MOVE zmm_prep_roledes-brief_desc TO role_desc.
1593         ENDIF.
1594       ENDIF.
1595
1596     ENDIF.
1597
1598   ENDMODULE.                    "TABLCTRL110_move OUTPUT

1638         """""""""""""""""""""""""""""

2258   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2259   *&      Module  value_list1  OUTPUT
2260   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2261   *       text
2262   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2263   MODULE value_list1 OUTPUT.
2264     SUPPRESS DIALOG.
2265     LEAVE TO LIST-PROCESSING AND RETURN TO SCREEN 0.
2266
2267     DATA : l_desc(30).
2268
2269     SORT ist_item DESCENDING.
2270
2271     LOOP AT ist_item INTO wa_item.
2272       CASE wa_item-moduleid.
2273         WHEN 'MM'.
2274           PERFORM check_module_status_mm.
2275         WHEN 'PM'.
2276           PERFORM check_module_status_pm.
2277         WHEN 'PS'.
2278           PERFORM check_module_status_ps.
2279         WHEN 'PP'.
2280           PERFORM check_module_status_pp.
2281         WHEN 'SD'.
2282           PERFORM check_module_status_sd.
2283         WHEN 'QM'.
2284           PERFORM check_module_status_qm.
2285         WHEN 'HSE'.
2286           PERFORM check_module_status_hse.
2287       ENDCASE.
2288     ENDLOOP.
2289
2290     LOOP AT ist_item INTO wa_item.
2291
2292       CASE wa_item-moduleid .
2293
2294         WHEN 'MM'.
2295
2296           AT NEW moduleid.
2297
2298             WRITE :/.
2299
2300             IF mm_not_ok = 'X'.
2301               FORMAT INTENSIFIED ON COLOR 6.
2302             ELSE.
2303               FORMAT INTENSIFIED ON COLOR 5.
2304             ENDIF.
2305
2306             WRITE: / 'MM Module', 'Role', 'Description',
2307                    AT 48  'Plant',
2308                    AT 53  'PurGp',
2309                    AT 59  'Sloc',
2310                    AT 64  'RecptLoc',
2311                    AT 73  'User level' .
2312
2313             FORMAT INTENSIFIED OFF COLOR OFF.
2314
2315   *     uline.
2316
2317           ENDAT.
2318
2319           IF zic_prep_rolereq-crc_fl = 'X'.
2320
2321             SELECT BRIEF_DESC FROM ZMM_PREP_ROLECRC INTO L_DESC UP TO 1 ROWS
2322    WHERE ROLE_TYPE = WA_ITEM-ROLE_NAME
2323    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
2324    ENDSELECT.
2325
2326           ELSE.
2327
2328             SELECT SINGLE brief_desc FROM zmm_prep_roledes INTO l_desc
2329                 WHERE role_type = wa_item-role_name.
2330
2331           ENDIF.
2332
2333           WRITE: / wa_item-moduleid, AT 12 wa_item-role_name, AT 17 l_desc,
2334                    AT 48 wa_item-plant,
2335                    AT 53 wa_item-grp,
2336                    AT 59 wa_item-sloc,
2337                    AT 64 wa_item-receipt_loc,
2338                    AT 73 wa_item-approver.
2339
2340         WHEN 'PM'.
2341
2342           AT NEW moduleid.
2343
2344             WRITE /.
2345
2346             IF pm_not_ok = 'X'.
2347               FORMAT INTENSIFIED ON COLOR 6.
2348             ELSE.
2349               FORMAT INTENSIFIED ON COLOR 5.
2350             ENDIF.
2351             WRITE: / 'PM Module', 'Role', 'Description',
2352                AT 48  'Plant',
2353                AT 54  'ShopNo'.
2354
2355             FORMAT INTENSIFIED OFF COLOR OFF.
2356
2357   *        uline.
2358           ENDAT.
2359
2360           SELECT SINGLE brief_desc FROM zpm_prep_roledes INTO l_desc
2361                 WHERE role_type = wa_item-role_name.
2362
2363           WRITE: / wa_item-moduleid, AT 12 wa_item-role_name, AT 17 l_desc,
2364                    AT 48 wa_item-plant,
2365                    AT 54 wa_item-shop_no.

4032   *  data : l_dynnr like sy-dynnr.

4426   MODULE change_srno12 INPUT.

4434     DESCRIBE TABLE g_tablctrl112_itab  LINES tablctrl112-lines.
4435     CLEAR g_srno.

4457             sign(1),
4458             option(2),

4460             high      LIKE zic_prep_rolerei-role_name,
4461           END OF seltab.

4478         dynnr = '0112'
4479       IMPORTING
4480         value = l_service.

4482     IF l_service = 'P1' OR l_service = 'P2' OR l_service = 'P3' OR l_service = 'PS'.
4483
4484       IF l_service = 'P1'.
4485         CONCATENATE  'BU' '%' INTO g_fldval.   " PRA Module changes
4486       ELSEIF l_service = 'P2'.
4487         CONCATENATE  'APP' '%' INTO g_fldval.   " PRA Module changes
4488       ELSEIF l_service = 'P3'.
4489         CONCATENATE  'CP' '%' INTO g_fldval.   " PRA Module changes
4490       ELSEIF l_service = 'PS'.
4491         CONCATENATE  'PS' '%' INTO g_fldval.   " PRA Module changes
4492       ENDIF.

4494                   TABLE it_role WHERE role_type LIKE g_fldval.
4495
4496     ELSE.
4497       SELECT * FROM zps_prep_serv_rl INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF
4498             TABLE it_role WHERE service = l_service.
4499
4500       LOOP AT it_role.
4501
4502         seltab-sign   = 'I'.
4503         seltab-option = 'EQ'.
4504         seltab-low    = it_role-role_type.
4505         APPEND seltab.
4506
4507       ENDLOOP.
4508
4509       SELECT * FROM zps_prep_roledes INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF
4510                   TABLE it_role WHERE role_type IN seltab.
4511     ENDIF.
4512
4513
4514     SORT it_role ASCENDING BY sort_field.
4515
4516     IF old_ok_code <> 'DISPLAY'.
4517
4518       CLEAR zic_prep_rolerei-role_name.

4520     ENDIF.

4523       dis_flag = 'X'.
4524     ENDIF.

4528     APPEND g_field_wa TO g_field_tab.
4529     g_field_wa-tabname = 'ZPS_PREP_ROLEDES'.
4530     g_field_wa-fieldname = 'BRIEF_DESC'.
4531     APPEND g_field_wa TO g_field_tab.
4532     g_field_wa-tabname = 'ZPS_PREP_ROLEDES'.
4533     g_field_wa-fieldname = 'DETAIL_DESC1'.
4534     APPEND g_field_wa TO g_field_tab.
4535     g_field_wa-tabname = 'ZPS_PREP_ROLEDES'.
4536     g_field_wa-fieldname = 'DETAIL_DESC2'.
4537     APPEND g_field_wa TO g_field_tab.

241    *&      Form  USER_OK_TC                                               *
242    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
243    FORM USER_OK_TC USING    P_TC_NAME TYPE DYNFNAM
244                             P_TABLE_NAME
245                             P_MARK_NAME
246                    CHANGING P_OK      LIKE SY-UCOMM.
247
248    *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
249      DATA: L_OK     TYPE SY-UCOMM,
250            L_OFFSET TYPE I.
251    *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
252
253    * Table control specific operations                                    *
254    *   evaluate TC name and operations                                    *
255      SEARCH P_OK FOR P_TC_NAME.
256      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
257        EXIT.
258      ENDIF.
259      L_OFFSET = STRLEN( P_TC_NAME ) + 1.
260      L_OK = P_OK+L_OFFSET.
261    * execute general and TC specific operations                           *
262      CASE L_OK.
263        WHEN 'INSR'.                      "insert row
264          PERFORM FCODE_INSERT_ROW USING    P_TC_NAME
265                                            P_TABLE_NAME.
266          CLEAR P_OK.
267          G_INS_FLAG = 'X'.
268
269        WHEN 'DELE'.                      "delete row
270
271          PERFORM FCODE_DELETE_ROW USING    P_TC_NAME
272                                            P_TABLE_NAME
273                                            P_MARK_NAME.
274          CLEAR P_OK.
275
276        WHEN 'P--' OR                     "top of list
277             'P-'  OR                     "previous page
278             'P+'  OR                     "next page
279             'P++'.                       "bottom of list
280          PERFORM COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC USING P_TC_NAME
281                                                L_OK.
282          CLEAR P_OK.
283    *     WHEN 'L--'.                       "total left
284    *       PERFORM FCODE_TOTAL_LEFT USING P_TC_NAME.
285    *
286    *     WHEN 'L-'.                        "column left
287    *       PERFORM FCODE_COLUMN_LEFT USING P_TC_NAME.
288    *
289    *     WHEN 'R+'.                        "column right
290    *       PERFORM FCODE_COLUMN_RIGHT USING P_TC_NAME.
291    *
292    *     WHEN 'R++'.                       "total right
293    *       PERFORM FCODE_TOTAL_RIGHT USING P_TC_NAME.
294    *
295        WHEN 'MARK'.                      "mark all filled lines
296          PERFORM FCODE_TC_MARK_LINES USING P_TC_NAME
297                                            P_TABLE_NAME
298                                            P_MARK_NAME   .
299          CLEAR P_OK.
300
301        WHEN 'DMRK'.                      "demark all filled lines
302          PERFORM FCODE_TC_DEMARK_LINES USING P_TC_NAME
303                                              P_TABLE_NAME
304                                              P_MARK_NAME .
305          CLEAR P_OK.
306
307    *     WHEN 'SASCEND'   OR
308    *          'SDESCEND'.                  "sort column
309    *       PERFORM FCODE_SORT_TC USING P_TC_NAME
310    *                                   l_ok.
311
312      ENDCASE.
313
314    ENDFORM.                              " USER_OK_TC
315
316    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
317    *&      Form  FCODE_INSERT_ROW                                         *
318    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
319    FORM FCODE_INSERT_ROW
320                  USING    P_TC_NAME           TYPE DYNFNAM
321                           P_TABLE_NAME             .
322
323    *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
324      DATA L_LINES_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
325      DATA L_SELLINE          LIKE SY-STEPL.
326      DATA L_LASTLINE         TYPE I.
327      DATA L_LINE             TYPE I.
328      DATA L_TABLE_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
329      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>                 TYPE CXTAB_CONTROL.
330      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TABLE>              TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
331      FIELD-SYMBOLS <LINES>              TYPE I.
332    *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
333
334      ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
335
336    * get the table, which belongs to the tc                               *
337      CONCATENATE P_TABLE_NAME '[]' INTO L_TABLE_NAME. "table body
338      ASSIGN (L_TABLE_NAME) TO <TABLE>.                "not headerline
339
340    * get looplines of TableControl
341      CONCATENATE 'G_' P_TC_NAME '_LINES' INTO L_LINES_NAME.
342      ASSIGN (L_LINES_NAME) TO <LINES>.
343
344    * get current line
345      GET CURSOR LINE L_SELLINE.
346      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.                   " append line to table
347        L_SELLINE = <TC>-LINES + 1.
348    *&SPWIZARD: set top line and new cursor line                           *
349        IF L_SELLINE > <LINES>.
350          <TC>-TOP_LINE = L_SELLINE - <LINES> + 1 .
351        ELSE.
352          <TC>-TOP_LINE = 1.
353        ENDIF.
354      ELSE.                               " insert line into table
355        L_SELLINE = <TC>-TOP_LINE + L_SELLINE - 1.
356        L_LASTLINE = <TC>-TOP_LINE + <LINES> - 1.
357      ENDIF.
358    *&SPWIZARD: set new cursor line                                        *
359      L_LINE = L_SELLINE - <TC>-TOP_LINE + 1.
360    * insert initial line
361      INSERT INITIAL LINE INTO <TABLE> INDEX L_SELLINE.
362      <TC>-LINES = <TC>-LINES + 1.
363    * set cursor
364      SET CURSOR LINE L_LINE.
365
366      G_I = L_LINE.
367      G_FIELD = 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-ROLE_NAME'.
368
369    ENDFORM.                              " FCODE_INSERT_ROW
370
371    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
372    *&      Form  FCODE_DELETE_ROW                                         *
373    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
374    FORM FCODE_DELETE_ROW
375                  USING    P_TC_NAME           TYPE DYNFNAM
376                           P_TABLE_NAME
377                           P_MARK_NAME   .
378
379    *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
380      DATA L_TABLE_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
381
382      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>         TYPE CXTAB_CONTROL.
383      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TABLE>      TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
384      FIELD-SYMBOLS <WA>.
385      FIELD-SYMBOLS <MARK_FIELD>.
386    *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
387
388      ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
389
390    * get the table, which belongs to the tc                               *
391      CONCATENATE P_TABLE_NAME '[]' INTO L_TABLE_NAME. "table body
392      ASSIGN (L_TABLE_NAME) TO <TABLE>.                "not headerline
393
394    * delete marked lines                                                  *
395      DESCRIBE TABLE <TABLE> LINES <TC>-LINES.
396
397      LOOP AT <TABLE> ASSIGNING <WA>.
398
399    *   access to the component 'FLAG' of the table header                 *
400        ASSIGN COMPONENT P_MARK_NAME OF STRUCTURE <WA> TO <MARK_FIELD>.
401
402    ***
403        DATA : L_I LIKE SY-INDEX.
404        L_I = 36.
405        IF <MARK_FIELD> = 'X' AND <WA>+L_I(1) = ''.
406          DELETE <TABLE> INDEX SYST-TABIX.
407          IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
408            <TC>-LINES = <TC>-LINES - 1.
409          ENDIF.
410        ENDIF.
411      ENDLOOP.
412
413    ENDFORM.                              " FCODE_DELETE_ROW
414
415    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
416    *&      Form  COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC
417    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
418    *       text
419    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
420    *      -->P_TC_NAME  name of tablecontrol
421    *      -->P_OK       ok code
422    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
423    FORM COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC USING    P_TC_NAME
424                                          P_OK.
425    *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
426      DATA L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE     TYPE I.
427      DATA L_TC_NAME             LIKE FELD-NAME.
428      DATA L_TC_LINES_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
429      DATA L_TC_FIELD_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
430
431      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>         TYPE CXTAB_CONTROL.
432      FIELD-SYMBOLS <LINES>      TYPE I.
433    *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
434
435      ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
436    * get looplines of TableControl
437      CONCATENATE 'G_' P_TC_NAME '_LINES' INTO L_TC_LINES_NAME.
438      ASSIGN (L_TC_LINES_NAME) TO <LINES>.
439    ***********************************************************************
440      G_TC_LINES = <TC>-LINES.
441    ***********************************************************************
442
443    * is no line filled?                                                   *
444      IF <TC>-LINES = 0.
445    *   yes, ...                                                           *
446        L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE = 1.
447      ELSE.
448    *   no, ...                                                            *
449        CALL FUNCTION 'SCROLLING_IN_TABLE'
450          EXPORTING
451            ENTRY_ACT      = <TC>-TOP_LINE
452            ENTRY_FROM     = 1
453            ENTRY_TO       = <TC>-LINES
454            LAST_PAGE_FULL = 'X'
455            LOOPS          = <LINES>
456            OK_CODE        = P_OK
457            OVERLAPPING    = 'X'
458          IMPORTING
459            ENTRY_NEW      = L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE
460          EXCEPTIONS
461    *       NO_ENTRY_OR_PAGE_ACT  = 01
462    *       NO_ENTRY_TO    = 02
463    *       NO_OK_CODE_OR_PAGE_GO = 03
464            OTHERS         = 0.
465      ENDIF.
466
467    * get actual tc and column                                             *
468      GET CURSOR FIELD L_TC_FIELD_NAME
469                 AREA  L_TC_NAME.
470
471      IF SYST-SUBRC = 0.
472        IF L_TC_NAME = P_TC_NAME.
473    *     set actual column                                                *
474          SET CURSOR FIELD L_TC_FIELD_NAME LINE 1.
475        ENDIF.
476      ENDIF.
477
478    * set the new top line                                                 *
479      <TC>-TOP_LINE = L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE.
480
481
482    ENDFORM.                              " COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC
483
484    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
485    *&      Form  FCODE_TC_MARK_LINES
486    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
487    *       marks all TableControl lines
488    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
489    *      -->P_TC_NAME  name of tablecontrol
490    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
491    FORM FCODE_TC_MARK_LINES USING P_TC_NAME
492                                   P_TABLE_NAME
493                                   P_MARK_NAME.
494    *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
495      DATA L_TABLE_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
496
497      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>         TYPE CXTAB_CONTROL.
498      FIELD-SYMBOLS <TABLE>      TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
499      FIELD-SYMBOLS <WA>.
500      FIELD-SYMBOLS <MARK_FIELD>.
501    *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
502
503      ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
504
505    * get the table, which belongs to the tc                               *
506      CONCATENATE P_TABLE_NAME '[]' INTO L_TABLE_NAME. "table body
507      ASSIGN (L_TABLE_NAME) TO <TABLE>.                "not headerline
508
509    * mark all filled lines                                                *
510      LOOP AT <TABLE> ASSIGNING <WA>.
511
512    *   access to the component 'FLAG' of the table header                 *
513        ASSIGN COMPONENT P_MARK_NAME OF STRUCTURE <WA> TO <MARK_FIELD>.
514
515        <MARK_FIELD> = 'X'.
516      ENDLOOP.
517    ENDFORM.                                          "fcode_tc_mark_lines
518

553    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
554    *&      Form  HELP_LIST
555    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
556    *       text
557    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
558    *  -->  p1        text
559    *  <--  p2        text
560    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
561    FORM HELP_LIST.
562
563      IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE IS INITIAL.
564        SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE'.
565        MESSAGE I082(ZHELP).
566        LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
567      ENDIF.
568      REFRESH : IT_COND.
569      CONCATENATE 'FICTR'  'LIKE'  INTO G_LINE SEPARATED BY
570      SPACE.
571      CONCATENATE G_LINE+0(10) '''' ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE '%' ''''  INTO
572                  G_LINE.
573      APPEND G_LINE TO IT_COND.
574      IF HELP_LIST_FLAG <> 'X' .
575        SELECT * FROM M_FISTB INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE IT_M_FISTB
576                      WHERE (IT_COND).
577
578    *Begin of <RD1K963151>.
579        IT_M_FISTB1[] = IT_M_FISTB[].
580        CLEAR IT_M_FISTB[].
581
582        SELECT FIKRS HIVARNT FISTL HIROOT_ST PARENT_ST  NEXT_ST CHILD_ST HILEVEL FROM
583               FMHISV INTO TABLE IT_FMHISV FOR ALL ENTRIES IN IT_M_FISTB1 WHERE
584               FISTL = IT_M_FISTB1-FICTR.
585
586        DELETE IT_FMHISV WHERE PARENT_ST = SPACE OR PARENT_ST = 'ONGC'
587        OR  PARENT_ST = 'OVL' OR PARENT_ST = 'OBV'.
588
589        LOOP AT IT_M_FISTB1 INTO WA_FISTB1.
590          READ TABLE IT_FMHISV WITH KEY FISTL = WA_FISTB1-FICTR.
591          IF SY-SUBRC = 0 .
592            MOVE WA_FISTB1-FICTR TO WA_FISTB-FICTR.
593            MOVE WA_FISTB1-BEZEICH TO WA_FISTB-BEZEICH.
594            APPEND WA_FISTB TO IT_M_FISTB.
595          ENDIF.
596        ENDLOOP.
597    *End of <RD1K963151>.
598
599        HELP_LIST_FLAG = 'X'.
600        REFRESH IT_COND.
601      ENDIF.
602      LOOP AT IT_M_FISTB INTO WA_M_FISTB.
603    *
604        IF WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC OR
605           WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC2 OR
606           WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC3 OR
607           WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC4.
608          WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK = 'X'.
609        ENDIF.
610
611        IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'DISPLAY' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE'.
612          IF WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK = 'X'.
613            WRITE: / WA_M_FISTB-FICTR, WA_M_FISTB-BEZEICH.
614          ENDIF.
615        ELSE.
616          WRITE: / WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK AS CHECKBOX, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR,
617                WA_M_FISTB-BEZEICH.
618        ENDIF.
619
620        HIDE : WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR.
621    *    CLEAR : wa_m_fistb-g_mark, wa_m_fistb-fictr.
622      ENDLOOP.
623      LINES = SY-LINNO .
624
625    ENDFORM.                    " HELP_LIST

630    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
631    *  -->  p1        text

633    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
634    FORM TICK_ALL.
635
636      SY-LSIND = 0.
637
638      MOVE 'REQ1' TO WA_TAB.
639      APPEND WA_TAB TO TAB.
640      MOVE 'SELALL' TO WA_TAB.
641      APPEND WA_TAB TO TAB.
642      MOVE 'DESELALL' TO WA_TAB.
643      APPEND WA_TAB TO TAB.
644
645      SET PF-STATUS 'STATUS_120' EXCLUDING TAB.
646      CLEAR : WA_TAB.
647      REFRESH : TAB.
648      WRITE :'Selected Values for Company Code :',ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE
649               COLOR COL_HEADING.
650      ULINE.
651
652
653      IF FLAG_S_FUNDC = 'X'.
654    *    refresh : s_fundc.
655        LOOP AT IT_M_FISTB INTO WA_M_FISTB.
656    *
657          WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK = 'X'.
658          WRITE: / WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK AS CHECKBOX, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR,
659                   WA_M_FISTB-BEZEICH.
660          MODIFY  IT_M_FISTB FROM WA_M_FISTB.
661          HIDE : WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR, WA_M_FISTB-BEZEICH.
662          CLEAR : WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR, WA_M_FISTB-BEZEICH.
663        ENDLOOP.
664
665        LINES = SY-LINNO .
666
667      ENDIF.
668
669
670
671    ENDFORM.                    " tick_all

717    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
718    *&      Form  pick
719    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
720    *       text
721    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
722    *  -->  p1        text
723    *  <--  p2        text
724    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
725    FORM PICK.
726
727      SY-LSIND = 0.
728
729      MOVE 'REQ1' TO WA_TAB.
730      APPEND WA_TAB TO TAB.
731      MOVE 'SELALL' TO WA_TAB.
732      APPEND WA_TAB TO TAB.
733      MOVE 'DESELALL' TO WA_TAB.
734      APPEND WA_TAB TO TAB.
735
736      DATA L_BLANK VALUE ''.
737
738      SET PF-STATUS 'STATUS_120' EXCLUDING TAB.
739      CLEAR : WA_TAB.
740      REFRESH : TAB.
741      WRITE :'Selected Values for Company Code :',ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE
742             COLOR COL_HEADING.
743      ULINE.
744
745
746      IF FLAG_S_FUNDC = 'X'.
747    *    refresh : s_fundc.
748        LOOP AT IT_M_FISTB INTO WA_M_FISTB.
749
750          LINES_INDEX = SY-TABIX + 4.
751
752          READ LINE LINES_INDEX FIELD VALUE WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK.
753
754          WRITE: / WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK AS CHECKBOX, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR,
755                   WA_M_FISTB-BEZEICH.
756
757          IF WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK <> 'X'.
758
759            IF WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC.
760              ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC = 'X'.
761            ENDIF.
762
763            IF WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC2.
764              CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC2.
765            ENDIF.
766
767            IF WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC3.
768              CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC3.
769            ENDIF.
770
771            IF WA_M_FISTB-FICTR = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC4.
772              CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC4.
773            ENDIF.
774
775          ENDIF.
776
777          MODIFY  IT_M_FISTB FROM WA_M_FISTB.
778          HIDE : WA_M_FISTB-G_MARK, WA_M_FISTB-FICTR.
779    *      CLEAR : wa_m_fistb-g_mark, wa_m_fistb-fictr, wa_m_fistb-bezeich.
780        ENDLOOP.
781
782        HELP_LIST_FLAG = 'X'.
783
784        LINES = SY-LINNO .
785
786        READ TABLE IT_M_FISTB INTO WA_M_FISTB WITH KEY G_MARK = 'X'.
787
788        IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
789
790          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC = WA_M_FISTB-FICTR.
791
792        ELSE.
793
794          CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC .
795
796        ENDIF.
797
798      ENDIF.
799
800    ENDFORM.                    " pick

814    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
815    *&      Form  Save_request
816    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
817    *       text
818    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
819    *  -->  p1        text
820    *  <--  p2        text
821    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
822    FORM SAVE_REQUEST.
823
824      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
825         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
826         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
827
828        PERFORM GEN_NO.
829
830      ENDIF.
831
832      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' OR
833         OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE'.
834
835        G_RELEASE = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_CR_FL.
836        G_APPROVE = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP_FL.
837        G_APPROVE0 = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP0_FL.
838        G_APPROVE1 = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP1_FL.
839
840
841        SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ
842                        WHERE DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
843
844        IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_CR_FL IS INITIAL.
845          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_CR_FL = G_RELEASE.
846        ENDIF.
847        IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP_FL IS INITIAL.
848          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP_FL = G_APPROVE.
849        ENDIF.
850        IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP1_FL IS INITIAL.
851          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP1_FL = G_APPROVE1.
852        ENDIF.
853
854        IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP0_FL IS INITIAL.
855          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP0_FL = G_APPROVE0.
856        ENDIF.
857
858
859        CLEAR : G_RELEASE, G_APPROVE, G_APPROVE0, G_APPROVE1.
860
861        IF G_RELEASE = 'X' AND ( G_APPROVE <> 'X' AND
862                                 G_APPROVE0 <> 'X' AND
863                                 G_APPROVE1 <> 'X' ).
864
865          G_APP_REL = 'X'.
866
867        ENDIF.
868
869      ENDIF.
870
871      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
872        MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
873      ELSE.
874        PERFORM INSERT_HEADER.
875      ENDIF.
876
877    ENDFORM.                    " Save_request
878    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
879    *&      Form  gen_no
880    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
881    *       text
882    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
883    *  -->  p1        text
884    *  <--  p2        text
885    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
886    FORM GEN_NO.
887
888      CALL FUNCTION 'NUMBER_GET_NEXT'
889        EXPORTING
890          NR_RANGE_NR = '01'
891          OBJECT      = 'ZDOCNUMB'
892        IMPORTING
893          NUMBER      = ZDOCNUMB.
894      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
895      ENDIF.
896
897    ENDFORM.                    " gen_no

1782   FORM EXIT_CONFIRM.
1783
1784     DATA L_CHOICE1.
1785     CLEAR L_CHOICE1.
1786
1787     IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
1788        OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
1789        OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE' OR
1790        OLD_OK_CODE = 'DELETE' OR
1791        OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' OR
1792        OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE'.
1793
1794       " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
1795   *    CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
1796   *         EXPORTING
1797   *              TEXTLINE1      = 'Data will be lost, Want to quit? '
1798   *              TITEL          = 'EXIT'
1799   *              START_COLUMN   = 25
1800   *              START_ROW      = 6
1801   *              CANCEL_DISPLAY = ''
1802   **                 DEFAULTOPTION = 'N'
1803   *         IMPORTING
1804   *              ANSWER         = l_choice1.
1805
1806       DATA : L_GET2(1) TYPE C.
1807       CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
1808         EXPORTING
1809           TITLEBAR              = 'EXIT '
1810           TEXT_QUESTION         = 'Data will be lost, Want to quit? '
1811           DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
1812           DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
1813           START_COLUMN          = 25
1814           START_ROW             = 6
1815         IMPORTING
1816           ANSWER                = L_GET2
1817         EXCEPTIONS
1818           TEXT_NOT_FOUND        = 1
1819           OTHERS                = 2.
1820       IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
1821         CASE L_GET2.
1822           WHEN '1'.
1823             MOVE 'J' TO L_CHOICE1.
1824           WHEN '2'.
1825             MOVE 'N' TO L_CHOICE1.
1826         ENDCASE.
1827       ENDIF.
1828       " End of <RD1K960036>.
1829
1830       IF L_CHOICE1 = 'J'.
1831         CLEAR L_CHOICE1.
1832         PERFORM CLEAR.
1833         PERFORM UNLOCK_RECORD.
1834         CALL SCREEN 100.
1835       ELSE.
1836       ENDIF.
1837
1838     ELSE.
1839
1840       PERFORM CLEAR.
1841       PERFORM UNLOCK_RECORD.
1842       CALL SCREEN 100.
1843
1844     ENDIF.
1845
1846
1847   ENDFORM.                    " exit_confirm

2059   FORM SAVE_CORS_TEXT.
2060
2061     DATA: L_THEADER LIKE THEAD.
2062     DATA: L_DATECH(10) TYPE C.
2063   ***********Assignments***********************
2064     CLEAR L_THEADER.
2065     L_THEADER-TDOBJECT   = 'ZHELP'.
2066     L_THEADER-TDID       = '0001'.
2067     L_THEADER-TDSPRAS    =  SY-LANGU.
2068     L_THEADER-TDLINESIZE =  72.
2069     MOVE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO TO L_THEADER-TDNAME.
2070     APPEND LINES OF TLINETAB2 TO TLINETAB1.
2071   *********************************************
2072     IF NOT TLINETAB1[] IS INITIAL.
2073       CLEAR G_CORES_SENDER.
2074       CONCATENATE SY-DATUM+6(2) '/'
2075                   SY-DATUM+4(2) '/'
2076                   SY-DATUM+0(4) INTO L_DATECH.
2077       CONCATENATE '**Reply' L_DATECH SY-UNAME INTO G_CORES_SENDER
2078        SEPARATED BY '          '.
2079       IF NOT TLINETAB2[] IS INITIAL.
2080         APPEND G_CORES_SENDER TO TLINETAB1.
2081       ENDIF.
2082       CLEAR G_CORES_SENDER.
2083       CALL FUNCTION 'SAVE_TEXT'
2084         EXPORTING
2085           CLIENT          = SY-MANDT
2086           HEADER          = L_THEADER
2087           SAVEMODE_DIRECT = 'X'
2088         TABLES
2089           LINES           = TLINETAB1
2090         EXCEPTIONS
2091           ID              = 1
2092           LANGUAGE        = 2
2093           NAME            = 3
2094           OBJECT          = 4
2095           OTHERS          = 5.
2096
2097       IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
2098         MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
2099                 WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
2100       ENDIF.
2101     ENDIF.
2102
2103   ENDFORM.                    " save_cors_text

2512   *  <--  p2        text
2513   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2514   FORM DELETE_REQUEST.
2515
2516     DATA : L_CHOICE.
2517     " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
2518   *    CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
2519   *         EXPORTING
2520   *              TEXTLINE1      = 'Are you sure, you want to delete the Document? '
2521   *              TITEL          = ''
2522   *              START_COLUMN   = 25
2523   *              START_ROW      = 6
2524   *              CANCEL_DISPLAY = ''
2525   *         IMPORTING
2526   *              ANSWER         = l_choice.
2527
2528     DATA : L_GET3(1) TYPE C.
2529     CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
2530       EXPORTING
2531         TEXT_QUESTION         = 'Are you sure, you want to delete the Document? '
2532         DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
2533         START_COLUMN          = 25
2534         START_ROW             = 6
2535       IMPORTING
2536         ANSWER                = L_GET3
2537       EXCEPTIONS
2538         TEXT_NOT_FOUND        = 1
2539         OTHERS                = 2.
2540     IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
2541       CASE L_GET3.
2542         WHEN '1'.
2543           MOVE 'J' TO L_CHOICE.
2544         WHEN '2'.
2545           MOVE 'N' TO L_CHOICE.
2546       ENDCASE.
2547     ENDIF.
2548     " End of <RD1K960036>.
2549
2550     IF L_CHOICE = 'J'.
2551       CLEAR L_CHOICE.
2552
2553   **************************************
2554
2555       ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
2556
2557       DELETE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ.
2558
2559       IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
2560
2561         PERFORM DELETE_ITEMS.
2562
2563
2564         IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-LONG_TEXT_FL <> ''.
2565           PERFORM DELETE_CORS_TEXT.
2566         ENDIF.
2567
2568         PERFORM CLEAR.
2569         PERFORM UNLOCK_RECORD.
2570         CALL SCREEN 100.
2571
2572       ELSE.
2573
2574         MESSAGE I057(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
2575
2576       ENDIF.
2577
2578     ELSE.
2579
2580     ENDIF.
2581
2582   ENDFORM.                    " delete_request

2591   FORM DELETE_ITEMS.
2592
2593     LOOP AT G_TABCTRL100_ITAB INTO G_TABCTRL100_WA.
2594
2595       MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TABCTRL100_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
2596       WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
2597       APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
2598
2599     ENDLOOP.
2600
2601     DELETE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
2602
2603     IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
2604       MESSAGE I120(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
2605     ENDIF.
2606
2607   ENDFORM.                    " delete_items

2641   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

2644   *       text
2645   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2646   *  -->  p1        text
2647   *  <--  p2        text
2648   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2649   FORM VERIFY.
2650
2651     DATA L_CHOICE.
2652     CLEAR L_CHOICE.
2653     " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
2654   *  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
2655   *      EXPORTING
2656   *               TEXTLINE1      = 'Request already released Flags will be cancelled? '
2657   *           TITEL          = 'RESET'
2658   *           START_COLUMN   = 25
2659   *           START_ROW      = 6
2660   *           CANCEL_DISPLAY = ''
2661   *           DEFAULTOPTION = 'N'
2662   *      IMPORTING
2663   *           ANSWER         = l_choice.
2664
2665     DATA : L_GET5(1) TYPE C.
2666
2667     CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
2668       EXPORTING
2669         TITLEBAR              = 'RESET '
2670         TEXT_QUESTION         = 'Request already released Flags will be cancelled? '
2671         DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
2672         DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
2673         START_COLUMN          = 25
2674         START_ROW             = 6
2675       IMPORTING
2676         ANSWER                = L_GET5
2677       EXCEPTIONS
2678         TEXT_NOT_FOUND        = 1
2679         OTHERS                = 2.
2680     IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
2681       CASE L_GET5.
2682         WHEN '1'.
2683           MOVE 'J' TO L_CHOICE.
2684         WHEN '2'.
2685           MOVE 'N' TO L_CHOICE.
2686       ENDCASE.
2687     ENDIF.
2688     " End of <RD1K960036>.
2689
2690     IF L_CHOICE = 'J'.
2691
2692       CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_CR_FL.
2693       CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP_FL.
2694       CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP0_FL.
2695       CLEAR ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP1_FL.
2696       ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IC'.
2697       PERFORM SAVE_REQUEST.
2698   **20/03/2006
2699       G_APP_REL = 'X'.
2700       CLEAR L_CHOICE.
2701
2702     ELSE.
2703
2704       PERFORM CLEAR.
2705       PERFORM UNLOCK_RECORD.
2706       CALL SCREEN 100.
2707
2708     ENDIF.
2709
2710   ENDFORM.                    " verify

2800                         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE'  OR

2884   FORM VERIFY1.
2885
2886     DATA : L_CHOICE.
2887     CLEAR L_CHOICE.
2888     " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
2889   *  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
2890   *      EXPORTING
2891   *               TEXTLINE1      = 'If u cancel release, u can change data else go in display mode'
2892   *               TEXTLINE2      = '& just do correspondence without cancelling release'
2893   *           TITEL          = 'Do you want to cancel release?'
2894   *           START_COLUMN   = 25
2895   *           START_ROW      = 6
2896   *           CANCEL_DISPLAY = ''
2897   *           DEFAULTOPTION = 'N'
2898   *      IMPORTING
2899   *           ANSWER         = l_choice.
2900
2901     DATA : L_GET6(1) TYPE C.
2902
2903     CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
2904       EXPORTING
2905         TITLEBAR              = 'Do you want to cancel release? '
2906         TEXT_QUESTION         = 'If u cancel release, u can change data else go in display mode'
2907                                 & ' & just do correspondence without cancelling release.'
2908         DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
2909         DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
2910         START_COLUMN          = 25
2911         START_ROW             = 6
2912       IMPORTING
2913         ANSWER                = L_GET6
2914       EXCEPTIONS
2915         TEXT_NOT_FOUND        = 1
2916         OTHERS                = 2.
2917     IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
2918       CASE L_GET6.
2919         WHEN '1'.
2920           MOVE 'J' TO L_CHOICE.
2921         WHEN '2'.
2922           MOVE 'N' TO L_CHOICE.
2923       ENDCASE.
2924     ENDIF.
2925     " End of <RD1K960036>.
2926
2927     IF L_CHOICE = 'J'.
2928
2929       OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
2930       CLEAR L_CHOICE.
2931
2932     ELSE.
2933
2934       OLD_OK_CODE = 'DISPLAY'.
2935       CLEAR L_CHOICE.
2936
2937     ENDIF.
2938
2939   ENDFORM.                                                    " verify1

2941   *&      Form  check_tel

2943   *       text

2946   *  <--  p2        text
2947   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2948   FORM CHECK_TEL.
2949
2950     IF    ( ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'DISPLAY' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE' OR
2951            OLD_OK_CODE = 'DELETE'
2952            OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE' )
2953            AND G_HD_COPIED = 'X' )
2954            OR ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' ).
2955       DATA : TEL_LEN TYPE I.
2956       TEL_LEN = STRLEN( ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-TELNO ).
2957       IF  ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-TELNO CN ' 0123456789-'.
2958         MESSAGE I097(ZHELP).
2959         CALL SCREEN 100.
2960       ELSE.
2961         IF TEL_LEN < 7.
2962           MESSAGE I098(ZHELP).
2963           CALL SCREEN 100.
2964         ENDIF.
2965       ENDIF.
2966     ENDIF.
2967   ENDFORM.                    " check_tel

3399           LOOP AT IT_T001L INTO WA_T001L.
3400
3401             IF G_TABLCTRL110_WA-SLOC = WA_T001L-LGORT.
3402               LOC_FLAG = 'X'.
3403             ENDIF.
3404
3405           ENDLOOP.
3406
3407           IF LOC_FLAG = 'X'.
3408             CLEAR LOC_FLAG.
3409           ELSE.
3410   ** cab_ajit 07.02.2006
3411             G_E_FL = 'X'.
3412             G_FIELD = 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-SLOC'.
3413             ROLLBACK WORK.
3414             MESSAGE E073(ZHELP).
3415
3416           ENDIF.
3417
3418         ENDIF.
3419
3420
3421   ***************************
3422
3423         CLEAR WA_RECPT.
3424         REFRESH IT_RECPT.
3425
3426         IF ( G_TABLCTRL110_WA-ROLE_NAME = 'M12' OR
3427            G_TABLCTRL110_WA-ROLE_NAME = 'M17' ) AND
3428            NOT G_TABLCTRL110_WA-RECEIPT_LOC IS INITIAL.
3429
3430           SELECT * FROM ZMM_LOCATION INTO TABLE IT_RECPT.
3431
3432           IF G_TABLCTRL110_WA-ROLE_NAME = 'M12'.
3433
3434             LOOP AT IT_RECPT INTO WA_RECPT.
3435
3436               IF WA_RECPT-LOCCG <> 'RL'.
3437                 DELETE IT_RECPT.
3438               ENDIF.
3439
3440             ENDLOOP.
3441
3442           ENDIF.
3443
3444
3445           IF G_TABLCTRL110_WA-ROLE_NAME = 'M17'.
3446
3447             LOOP AT IT_RECPT INTO WA_RECPT.
3448
3449               IF WA_RECPT-LOCCG <> 'CF'.
3450                 DELETE IT_RECPT.
3451               ENDIF.
3452
3453             ENDLOOP.
3454
3455           ENDIF.
3456
3457         ENDIF.
3458
3459         IF  NOT G_TABLCTRL110_WA-RECEIPT_LOC IS INITIAL.
3460
3461           LOOP AT IT_RECPT INTO WA_RECPT.
3462
3463             IF G_TABLCTRL110_WA-RECEIPT_LOC = WA_RECPT-LOCCD.
3464               LOC_FLAG = 'X'.
3465             ENDIF.
3466
3467           ENDLOOP.
3468
3469           IF LOC_FLAG = 'X'.
3470             CLEAR LOC_FLAG.
3471           ELSE.
3472             G_E_FL = 'X'.
3473             G_FIELD = 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-RECEIPT_LOC'.
3474             ROLLBACK WORK.
3475             MESSAGE E075(ZHELP).
3476
3477           ENDIF.
3478
3479         ENDIF.
3480
3481
3482   *****************************
3483
3484       ENDIF.
3485
3486     ENDLOOP.
3487
3488   ENDFORM.                    " validate_lineitem_datax

4240   FORM VALIDATE_ROLE_APPROVAL_LEVEL.
4241
4242   ** Check approval module wise & line item wise
4243
4244     SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP
4245          WHERE ROLE_TYPE = WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME.
4246
4247     IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
4248
4249       IF ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = 'L3' AND
4250                    G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L3'.
4251
4252       ELSEIF ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = 'IM' AND
4253                    G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L3'.
4254         G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'IM'.
4255       ELSEIF  ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = 'L1' AND
4256                    ( G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L3' OR
4257                      G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'IM' ).
4258         G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L1'.
4259       ENDIF.
4260
4261   **** CAB_AJIT Approval check added on 11/12/2006
4262       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE'.

4264
4265           IF SY-SUBRC = 0 AND OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE'.
4266             IF ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = G_USER
4267                OR ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER2 = G_USER
4268                OR ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER3 = G_USER
4269            """""""""""""""""""""""""""
4270              "added by lipsy for l2 approver on 20.03.2015 RD1K996555
4271               OR ( MODULEID = 'SRM' AND ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = G_USER_L2 )
4272                "end of addition by lipsy for l2 approver on 20.03.2015 RD1K996555
4273             """""""""""""""""""""""""
4274               .
4275             ELSE.
4276
4277               IF OKCODE_100 = 'SAV'.
4278                 IF ERR_FLG <> 'X'.
4279                   ERR_FLG = 'X'.
4280                   CLEAR : SY-UCOMM, OKCODE_100.
4281                 ENDIF.
4282                 ROLLBACK WORK.
4283                 MESSAGE I047(ZHELP) WITH ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-ROLE_TYPE.
4284                 CLEAR OKCODE_100.
4285                 CALL SCREEN 100.
4286               ENDIF.
4287             ENDIF.
4288           ENDIF.

4290         ENDIF.

4720                         ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE' OR

4743             ROLLBACK WORK.

4745             ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE .
4746             CLEAR : OKCODE_100, SY-UCOMM.

5418   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5419   *&      Form  insert_items_pp
5420   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5421   *       text
5422   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5423   *  -->  p1        text
5424   *  <--  p2        text
5425   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5426   FORM INSERT_ITEMS_PP.
5427
5428     DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
5429     CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB, I.
5430
5431     SORT G_TABLCTRL113_ITAB
5432     BY ROLE_NAME PLANT SLOC RES CTF_SLOC.
5433
5434     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TABLCTRL113_ITAB
5435       COMPARING ROLE_NAME REJ_FL PLANT SLOC RES
5436       CTF_SLOC.
5437
5438     LOOP AT G_TABLCTRL113_ITAB INTO G_TABLCTRL113_WA.
5439
5440       MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TABLCTRL113_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
5441
5442   *    Perform check_items_save.
5443
5444       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
5445          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
5446          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
5447         WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
5448       ENDIF.
5449
5450       WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
5451       IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
5452         WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
5453       ENDIF.
5454       IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
5455         I = I + 1.
5456         WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
5457         APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
5458       ENDIF.
5459
5460       G_I = I.
5461
5462       PERFORM CHECK_MODULE_WISE.
5463
5464     ENDLOOP.
5465
5466     DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
5467
5468     IF G_LINES_RL = 0.
5469       ROLLBACK WORK.
5470       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
5471         IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
5472           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
5473           MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
5474         ENDIF.
5475       ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' .
5476         MESSAGE I103(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
5477       ELSE.
5478         ROLLBACK WORK.
5479       ENDIF.
5480     ELSE.
5481       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
5482         ROLLBACK WORK.
5483         MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
5484       ELSE.
5485
5486         IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO'
5487   .
5488         ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE <> 'DISPLAY'.
5489           DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE
5490           DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO AND
5491           MODULEID = MODULEID.
5492         ENDIF.
5493
5494         MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
5495
5496       ENDIF.
5497
5498     ENDIF.
5499
5500   ENDFORM.                    " insert_items_pp

5640          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
5641          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
5642         WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
5643       ENDIF.

5645       WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.

5647         WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.

5650         I = I + 1.

5652         APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.

5668           MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
5669         ENDIF.
5670       ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' .
5671         MESSAGE I103(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
5672       ELSE.
5673         ROLLBACK WORK.
5674       ENDIF.
5675     ELSE.
5676       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
5677         ROLLBACK WORK.
5678         MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
5679       ELSE.
5680
5681         IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO'
5682   .
5683         ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE <> 'DISPLAY'.
5684           DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE
5685           DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO AND
5686           MODULEID = MODULEID.
5687         ENDIF.
5688
5689         MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
5690
5691       ENDIF.
5692
5693     ENDIF.
5694
5695   ENDFORM.                    " insert_items_sd

6127   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
6128   *&      Form  insert_items_qm
6129   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
6130   *       text
6131   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
6132   *  -->  p1        text
6133   *  <--  p2        text
6134   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
6135   FORM INSERT_ITEMS_QM.
6136
6137     DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
6138     CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB, I.
6139
6140     SORT G_TABLCTRL115_ITAB
6141     BY ROLE_NAME PLANT ASSET_QM.
6142
6143     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TABLCTRL115_ITAB
6144       COMPARING ROLE_NAME REJ_FL PLANT ASSET_QM.
6145
6146     LOOP AT G_TABLCTRL115_ITAB INTO G_TABLCTRL115_WA.
6147
6148       MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TABLCTRL115_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
6149
6150   *    Perform check_items_save.
6151
6152       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
6153          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
6154          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
6155         WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
6156       ENDIF.
6157
6158       WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
6159       IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
6160         WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
6161       ENDIF.
6162       IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
6163         I = I + 1.
6164         WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
6165         APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
6166       ENDIF.
6167
6168       G_I = I.
6169
6170       PERFORM CHECK_MODULE_WISE.
6171
6172     ENDLOOP.
6173
6174     DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
6175
6176     IF G_LINES_RL = 0.
6177       ROLLBACK WORK.
6178       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
6179         IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
6180           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
6181           MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
6182         ENDIF.
6183       ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' .
6184         MESSAGE I103(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
6185       ELSE.
6186         ROLLBACK WORK.
6187       ENDIF.
6188     ELSE.
6189       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
6190         ROLLBACK WORK.
6191         MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
6192       ELSE.
6193
6194         IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO'
6195   .
6196         ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE <> 'DISPLAY'.
6197           DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE
6198           DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO AND
6199           MODULEID = MODULEID.
6200         ENDIF.
6201
6202         MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
6203
6204       ENDIF.
6205
6206     ENDIF.
6207
6208   ENDFORM.                    " insert_items_qm

6644   FORM INSERT_ITEMS_HS.
6645
6646     DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
6647     CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB.
6648
6649     SORT G_TABLCTRL116_ITAB
6650     BY ROLE_NAME.
6651
6652     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TABLCTRL116_ITAB
6653       COMPARING ROLE_NAME REJ_FL.
6654
6655     LOOP AT G_TABLCTRL116_ITAB INTO G_TABLCTRL116_WA.
6656
6657       MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TABLCTRL116_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
6658
6659   *    Perform check_items_save.
6660
6661       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
6662          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
6663          OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
6664         WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
6665       ENDIF.
6666
6667       WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
6668       IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
6669         WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
6670       ENDIF.
6671       IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
6672         I = I + 1.
6673         WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
6674         APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
6675       ENDIF.
6676
6677       G_I = I.
6678
6679       PERFORM CHECK_MODULE_WISE.
6680
6681     ENDLOOP.
6682
6683     DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
6684
6685     IF G_LINES_RL = 0.
6686       ROLLBACK WORK.
6687       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
6688         IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
6689           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
6690           MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
6691         ENDIF.
6692       ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' .
6693         MESSAGE I103(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
6694       ELSE.
6695         ROLLBACK WORK.
6696       ENDIF.
6697     ELSE.
6698       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
6699         ROLLBACK WORK.
6700         MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
6701       ELSE.
6702
6703         IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO'
6704   .
6705         ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE <> 'DISPLAY'.
6706           DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE
6707           DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO AND
6708           MODULEID = MODULEID.
6709         ENDIF.
6710
6711         MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
6712
6713       ENDIF.
6714
6715     ENDIF.
6716
6717
6718   ENDFORM.                    " insert_items_hs

6893   FORM INSERT_ITEMS_OLM .
6894     DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
6895     CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB.
6896
6897     SORT G_TC_117_ITAB
6898     BY ROLE_NAME.
6899
6900     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TC_117_ITAB
6901       COMPARING ROLE_NAME.
6902
6903     LOOP AT G_TC_117_ITAB INTO G_TC_117_WA.
6904
6905       MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TC_117_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
6906
6907       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
6908         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
6909         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
6910         WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
6911       ENDIF.
6912
6913       WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
6914       IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
6915         WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
6916       ENDIF.
6917       IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
6918         I = I + 1.
6919         WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
6920         APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
6921       ENDIF.
6922
6923       G_I = I.
6924     ENDLOOP.
6925
6926     DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
6927
6928     IF G_LINES_RL = 0.
6929       ROLLBACK WORK.
6930       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
6931         IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
6932           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
6933           MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
6934         ENDIF.
6935       ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' .
6936         MESSAGE I103(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
6937       ELSE.
6938         ROLLBACK WORK.
6939       ENDIF.
6940     ELSE.
6941       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
6942         ROLLBACK WORK.
6943         MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
6944       ELSE.
6945
6946         IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO'
6947   .
6948         ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE <> 'DISPLAY'.
6949           DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE
6950           DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO AND
6951           MODULEID = MODULEID.
6952         ENDIF.
6953
6954         MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
6955       ENDIF.
6956
6957     ENDIF.
6958   ENDFORM.                    " INSERT_ITEMS_OLM

7792     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM IT_ROLES1.
7793
7794     LOOP AT IT_ROLES1 INTO WA_ROLES1.
7795
7796       WRITE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FR_DATE_AUTH TO WA_DAT1 DD/MM/YYYY.
7797
7798       WRITE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-TO_DATE_AUTH TO WA_DAT2 DD/MM/YYYY.
7799
7800       WA_ROLES1-FR_DATE_AUTH = WA_DAT1.
7801       WA_ROLES1-TO_DATE_AUTH = WA_DAT2.
7802       MODIFY IT_ROLES1 FROM WA_ROLES1.
7803       CLEAR WA_ROLES1.
7804     ENDLOOP.
7805
7806
7807     PERFORM COPY_VALUES.
7808
7809     PERFORM INSERT_RECORD.
7810
7811     REFRESH IT_AGR.
7812     IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-RSN_CODE = '02' AND ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-OFF_ORDER_NO IS INITIAL.
7813       SET PARAMETER ID 'RCODE' FIELD ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-RSN_CODE.
7814       PERFORM DELIMIT_ROLES.
7815     ENDIF.
7816
7817     PERFORM SAVE_REQUEST_ASSIGN.
7818
7819     GL_ANS = GL_ANS_SAVE.
7820     CLEAR GL_ANS_SAVE.
7821
7822     PERFORM FINAL_PROCESS.
7823     CLEAR : FLAG, FLAG1.
7824
7825   ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_ROLES

7832   *  <--  p2        text
7833   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
7834   FORM CREATE_ROLES_OLM .
7835     CLEAR IT_ROLES0.
7836     CLEAR IT_ROLES1.
7837
7838     REFRESH: IT_ROLES_OLM[].
7839
7840     SELECT * FROM ZHELP_OLMROLES INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE
7841      IT_ROLES_OLM.
7842
7843     LOOP AT IT_ROLES_OLM INTO WA_ROLES_OLM.
7844       APPEND WA_ROLES_OLM TO IT_ROLES0.
7845     ENDLOOP.
7846
7847     CLEAR WA_ROLES_OLM.
7848     LOOP AT IT_ROLES0 INTO WA_ROLES_OLM.
7849
7850       IF NOT WA_ROLES_OLM-ROLE_TYPE IS INITIAL.
7851
7852         LOOP AT  G_TC_117_ITAB INTO WA_ROLESZ_OLM.
7853           IF WA_ROLES_OLM-ROLE_TYPE = WA_ROLESZ_OLM-ROLE_NAME .
7854             WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
7855             WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ROLES_OLM-ROLE_NAME.
7856             APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
7857           ENDIF.
7858         ENDLOOP.
7859
7860       ENDIF.
7861
7862     ENDLOOP.
7863
7864     SORT IT_ROLES1.
7865
7866     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM IT_ROLES1.
7867
7868     LOOP AT IT_ROLES1 INTO WA_ROLES1.
7869
7870       WRITE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-FR_DATE_AUTH TO WA_DAT1 DD/MM/YYYY.
7871
7872       WRITE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-TO_DATE_AUTH TO WA_DAT2 DD/MM/YYYY.
7873
7874       WA_ROLES1-FR_DATE_AUTH = WA_DAT1.
7875       WA_ROLES1-TO_DATE_AUTH = WA_DAT2.
7876       MODIFY IT_ROLES1 FROM WA_ROLES1.
7877       CLEAR WA_ROLES1.
7878     ENDLOOP.
7879
7880     PERFORM COPY_VALUES.
7881
7882
7883     PERFORM INSERT_RECORD.
7884     PERFORM SAVE_REQUEST_ASSIGN.
7885     GL_ANS = GL_ANS_SAVE.
7886     CLEAR GL_ANS_SAVE.
7887
7888     PERFORM FINAL_PROCESS.
7889
7890
7891   ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_ROLES_OLM

8774         CLEAR ZUSRMST.
8775
8776         SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZUSRMST WHERE CPFNO =
8777                                    ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDCR.
8778
8779         IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
8780
8781         ELSE.
8782   *
8783           CONCATENATE ZUSRMST-FIRST_NAME ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME INTO
8784             ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
8785           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-NAMECR = ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
8786
8787         ENDIF.
8788
8789       ENDIF.
8790
8791     ENDIF.
8792
8793     IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDAP IS INITIAL.
8794
8795       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE' AND
8796             ( ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP_FL = 'X' ).
8797         ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDAP = SY-UNAME.
8798         ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-APP_DATE  = SY-DATUM.
8799
8800         CLEAR ZUSRMST.
8801
8802         SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZUSRMST WHERE CPFNO =
8803                               ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDAP.
8804
8805         IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
8806
8807         ELSE.
8808
8809           CONCATENATE ZUSRMST-FIRST_NAME ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME INTO
8810            ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
8811           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-NAMEAPP = ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
8812         ENDIF.
8813
8814       ENDIF.
8815
8816     ELSE.
8817
8818       IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE' AND
8819             ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP0_FL = 'X'
8820                   AND ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-REQ_APP1_FL = 'X'.
8821
8822         ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDAP = SY-UNAME.
8823         ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-APP_DATE = SY-DATUM.
8824
8825         SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZUSRMST WHERE CPFNO =
8826                                 ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDAP.
8827         IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
8828           MESSAGE E043(ZHELP).
8829         ELSE.
8830
8831           CONCATENATE ZUSRMST-FIRST_NAME ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME INTO
8832           ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
8833           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-NAMEAPP = ZUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
8834         ENDIF.
8835       ENDIF.
8836     ENDIF.

8838   *****************************
8839     DATA L_FUNDC_NO LIKE SY-INDEX.
8840     CLEAR L_FUNDC_NO.
8841
8842   *****************************
8843     IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS <> 'C'.
8844
8845       ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IF'.
8846
8847     ENDIF.
8848
8849   *****
8850     IF G_FUNDC_ERR_FLAG <> 'X'.
8851
8852   *************************************************************
8853
8854   ** Module wise check & insertion
8855
8856       CASE MODULEID.
8857
8858   *      WHEN 'MM'.
8859   *
8860   *        PERFORM insert_items_assign.
8861
8862         WHEN 'OLM'.
8863
8864           PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_OLM_ASSIGN.
8865
8866   *****************************************@
8867         WHEN OTHERS.
8868           PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_ASSIGN.
8869   *****************************************@
8870
8871       ENDCASE.
8872
8873       IF SY-TCODE <> 'ZIC_AUTH_CORETEAM'.
8874
8875         PERFORM ITEMS_APPROVAL_CHECK_ASSIGN.
8876
8877       ENDIF.
8878
8879   ***********************
8880
8881       IF SY-SUBRC = 0 AND ( ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS <> 'IC'
8882                           AND ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS <> 'IR' ).
8883
8884         SELECT * FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI INTO TABLE IST_ITEMTAB
8885                 WHERE DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
8886
8887         LOOP AT IST_ITEMTAB INTO WA_ITEMTAB.
8888           IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL = ''.
8889             IF WA_ITEMTAB-STATUS = '' AND
8890                 WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_REQUEST = ''.
8891               G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P  = 'X'.
8892             ELSEIF WA_ITEMTAB-STATUS = 'H'.
8893               G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H = 'X'.
8894             ELSEIF  WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_REQUEST <> ''.
8895               G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R = 'X'.
8896             ENDIF.
8897           ENDIF.
8898         ENDLOOP.
8899
8900         IF ( G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P  = 'X' OR
8901            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H  = 'X' ) AND
8902            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R = 'X'.
8903           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'PC'.
8904         ELSEIF G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P  <> 'X' AND
8905            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H  = 'X' AND
8906            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R = 'X'.
8907           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'PC'.
8908         ELSEIF G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P  = '' AND
8909            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H  = '' AND
8910            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R = 'X'.
8911           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'C'.
8912         ELSEIF G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P  = 'X' AND
8913            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H  <> 'X' AND
8914            G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R <> 'X'.
8915           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IF'.
8916         ELSEIF  G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P = '' AND
8917                  G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H = '' AND
8918                    G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R <> ''.
8919           ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'C'.
8920         ENDIF.
8921
8922       ENDIF.
8923
8924
8925
8926       MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ.
8927
8928       CLEAR : G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_P, G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_H,
8929               G_REQUEST_CLOSE_FLAG_R.
8930
8931
8932   ****Saving the long text.                              *****
8933
8934       IF ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' ) OR
8935          ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE' ) OR
8936          ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' ) OR
8937          ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE' ).
8938
8939         PERFORM SAVE_CORS_TEXT.
8940         PERFORM UNLOCK_RECORD.
8941       ENDIF.
8942
8943       IF G_ROLE_FLAG = 'X'.
8944         CLEAR G_ROLE_FLAG.
8945
8946
8947       ELSE.
8948
8949         IF L_OLD_OK_CODE = 'X'.
8950           SET PARAMETER ID 'ZOLDCODE' FIELD L_INITIAL.
8951           LEAVE PROGRAM.
8952         ELSE.
8953           PERFORM CLEAR_ASSIGN.
8954
8955         ENDIF.
8956
8957       ENDIF.
8958     ELSE.
8959     ENDIF.
8960   ENDFORM.                    " INSERT_HEADER_ASSIGN

9180   *assigned  &  completed - ' zic_prep_rolereq-docno into
9181

9183        'been assigned  &  completed - ' ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO INTO
9184   * end of <RD1K960036>
9185   OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE
9186   SEPARATED BY SPACE.
9187       APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
9188     ELSE.
9189   * begin of <RD1K960036>
9190   * Literal exceeding more then one line is not allowed
9191   *      concatenate 'Please check your role request which has been updated
9192   * - ' zic_prep_rolereq-docno into  object_content-line
9193   *    CONCATENATE 'Please check your role request which has been' &
9194   *     ' updated - ' zic_prep_rolereq-docno INTO  object_content-line
9195   ** end of <RD1K960036>
9196   *SEPARATED BY space.
9197   *    APPEND object_content.
9198     ENDIF.

9204   OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE
9205   SEPARATED BY SPACE.
9206       ENDIF.

9210

9275       EXPORTING

9280         SENT_TO_ALL                = SENT_TO_ALL
9281       TABLES

9283         OBJECT_CONTENT             = OBJECT_CONTENT
9284         RECEIVERS                  = RECEIVERS
9285       EXCEPTIONS

9287         DOCUMENT_NOT_SENT          = 02
9288         DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_EXIST    = 03
9289         OPERATION_NO_AUTHORIZATION = 04
9290         PARAMETER_ERROR            = 05
9291         X_ERROR                    = 06
9292         ENQUEUE_ERROR              = 07.

9294     CASE SY-SUBRC.
9295       WHEN 0.
9296
9297   *      MESSAGE i060(zhelp) WITH zic_prep_rolereq-useridcr.
9298       WHEN '01'.
9299         RAISE TOO_MANY_RECEIVERS.
9300       WHEN '02'.

9302       WHEN '03'.

9306       WHEN '05'.

9308       WHEN '06'.
9309         RAISE X_ERROR.
9310       WHEN '07'.

9317   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9318   *&      Form  INSERT_ITEMS_ASSIGN
9319   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9320   *       text
9321   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9322   *  -->  p1        text
9323   *  <--  p2        text
9324   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9325   FORM INSERT_ITEMS_ASSIGN .
9326     DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
9327     CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB.
9328
9329     CASE MODULEID.
9330       WHEN 'PM'.
9331
9332         PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_PM.
9333
9334       WHEN 'PS'.
9335
9336         PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_PS.
9337
9338       WHEN 'PP'.
9339
9340         PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_PP.
9341
9342       WHEN 'SD'.
9343
9344         PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_SD.
9345
9346       WHEN 'QM'.
9347
9348         PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_QM.
9349
9350       WHEN 'HSE'.
9351
9352         PERFORM INSERT_ITEMS_HS.
9353
9354       WHEN 'MM'.
9355         SORT G_TABLCTRL110_ITAB
9356         BY ROLE_NAME PLANT GRP  SLOC RECEIPT_LOC APPROVER.
9357
9358         DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TABLCTRL110_ITAB
9359           COMPARING ROLE_NAME PLANT GRP  SLOC RECEIPT_LOC APPROVER REJ_FL.
9360
9361         LOOP AT G_TABLCTRL110_ITAB INTO G_TABLCTRL110_WA.
9362
9363           MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TABLCTRL110_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
9364
9365           IF G_ROLE_FLAG = 'X' AND WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL = '' AND
9366               WA_ITEMTAB-STATUS = '' AND WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_REQUEST = ''.
9367             WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_REQUEST = ZROLEREQNO.
9368           ENDIF.
9369
9370           IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE'.
9371             WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
9372           ENDIF.
9373
9374           WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
9375           IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
9376             WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
9377           ENDIF.
9378           IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
9379             I = I + 1.
9380             WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
9381             APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
9382           ENDIF.
9383
9384           G_I = I.
9385
9386   *    PERFORM check_items_save_assign.
9387
9388         ENDLOOP.
9389
9390         DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
9391
9392         IF G_LINES_RL = 0.
9393           IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
9394             IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
9395               SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
9396               MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
9397             ENDIF.
9398           ELSE.
9399             ROLLBACK WORK.
9400           ENDIF.
9401         ELSE.
9402
9403           DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO
9404              AND MODULEID = MODULEID..
9405
9406           MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
9407
9408           IF SY-SUBRC = 0 AND G_ROLE_FLAG <> 'X'.
9409             MESSAGE I045(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
9410           ENDIF.
9411
9412         ENDIF.
9413
9414     ENDCASE.
9415   ENDFORM.                    " INSERT_ITEMS_ASSIGN

9672   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9673   FORM VALIDATE_ROLE_APPROVAL_AS .
9674
9675     SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP
9676          WHERE ROLE_TYPE = WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME.
9677
9678     IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
9679
9680       IF ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = 'L3' AND
9681                    G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L3'.
9682
9683       ELSEIF ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = 'IM' AND
9684                    G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L3'.
9685         G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'IM'.
9686       ELSEIF  ZMM_PREP_ROLEGRP-APPROVER1 = 'L1' AND
9687                    ( G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L3' OR
9688                      G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'IM' ).
9689         G_APPROVER_LEVEL = 'L1'.
9690       ENDIF.
9691
9692     ENDIF.
9693   ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_ROLE_APPROVAL_AS
