9711   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9712   *&      Form  WRITE_LIST
9713   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9714   *       text
9715   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9716   *  -->  p1        text
9717   *  <--  p2        text
9718   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9719   FORM WRITE_LIST .
9720     SET PF-STATUS 'STATUS_130' EXCLUDING 'SEL'.
9721
9722     READ TABLE IT_ROLES1 INTO WA_ROLES1 INDEX 1.  "#EC CI_NOORDER
9723     G_USERID = WA_ROLES1-USERID.
9724     L_COLOR = 5.
9725     LOOP AT IT_ROLES1 INTO WA_ROLES1.
9726       IF G_USERID = WA_ROLES1-USERID.
9727         WRITE : / WA_ROLES1-USERID COLOR 1,WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME COLOR 2.
9728       ELSE.
9729         WRITE : / WA_ROLES1-USERID COLOR 3,WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME COLOR 3.
9730       ENDIF.
9731       G_USERID = WA_ROLES1-USERID.
9732     ENDLOOP.
9733   ENDFORM.                    " WRITE_LIST
9734   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9735   *&      Form  FINAL_PROCESS
9736   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9737   *       text
9738   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9739   *  -->  p1        text
9740   *  <--  p2        text
9741   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9742   FORM FINAL_PROCESS .
9743
9744     SET PARAMETER ID 'ZROLEREQNO' FIELD ZROLEREQNO.
9745
9746     CLEAR:V_MODULEID.
9747
9748     V_MODULEID  = MODULEID.
9749
9750     EXPORT V_MODULEID TO MEMORY ID 'ID3'.
9751
9752     CLEAR V_REMARKS_HEAD.
9753     CONCATENATE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO ' - ARMS'
9754          ' - ' MODULEID ' Module' INTO V_REMARKS_HEAD.
9755     EXPORT V_REMARKS_HEAD TO MEMORY ID 'ID2'.
9756     CLEAR ZUSERID.
9757     MOVE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDCR TO ZUSERID.
9758     EXPORT ZUSERID TO MEMORY ID 'ID'.
9759     CLEAR ZAPPROVER.
9760     MOVE ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDAP TO ZAPPROVER.
9761     EXPORT ZAPPROVER TO MEMORY ID 'ID1'.
9762
9763     CLEAR:V_MESSAGE_AS.
9764     PERFORM ROLE_HELP.
9765     GET PARAMETER ID 'ZROLEREQNO' FIELD ZROLEREQNO.
9766
9767     IF NOT ZROLEREQNO IS INITIAL AND ZROLEREQNO <> '00000000'.
9768
9769       SUBMIT ZBC_ROLE_REP01_RFC AND RETURN.
9770
9771       MESSAGE I056(ZBC) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
9772
9773       G_ROLE_FLAG = 'X'.
9774       ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IR'.
9775
9776       PERFORM SAVE_REQUEST_ASSIGN.
9777       IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IF' OR
9778        ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'N'.
9779       ELSE.
9780         PERFORM SEND_SAPMAIL_ASSIGN .
9781
9782       ENDIF.
9783       PERFORM CLEAR_ASSIGN.
9784       REFRESH OBJECT_CONTENT.
9785     ENDIF.
9786     IF V_MESSAGE_AS = 'X'.
9787       PERFORM SAVE_REQUEST_ASSIGN.
9788       PERFORM SEND_SAPMAIL_ASSIGN .
9789
9790       V_MESSAGE_UNAS = 'All roles already assigned or do not exist.'.
9791
9792       MESSAGE I735(ZMM) WITH V_MESSAGE_UNAS.
9793     ELSE.
9794     ENDIF.
9795
9796     LEAVE PROGRAM.
9797
9798   ENDFORM.                    " FINAL_PROCESS
9799   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9800   *&      Form  ROLE_HELP
9801   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
9802   *       text
9803   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9804   *  -->  p1        text
9805   *  <--  p2        text
9806   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9807   FORM ROLE_HELP .
9808     DATA: UPL_COUNT TYPE I.
9809     DATA: XFLAG(1).
9810     DATA: XXCPF_NO LIKE ZAUTH_ITEM-CPF_NO.
9811     DATA: XXROLE LIKE ZAUTH_ITEM-ROLE.
9812
9813
9814     DATA: XXFROM_DAT LIKE ZAUTH_ITEM-FROM_DAT .
9815     DATA: XXTO_DAT LIKE ZAUTH_ITEM-TO_DAT .
9816     DATA: L_AGR_USERS LIKE TABLE OF AGR_USERS WITH HEADER LINE .
9817
9818
9819     DATA : DATEO(10), DATE LIKE SY-DATUM.
9820     DATA : ZUSERID LIKE ZAUTH_HEAD-REQUESTED_BY.
9821     DATA : ZAPPROVER LIKE ZAUTH_HEAD-APPROVED_BY.
9822     DATA:V_REMARKS_HEAD TYPE ZAUTH_HEAD-REMARKS,
9823          V_MODULEID(3).
9824
9825     ZAUTH_HEAD-AUTH_REQ_DATE = SY-DATUM.
9826
9827     IMPORT  V_MODULEID TO  V_MODULEID FROM MEMORY ID 'ID3'.
9828     ZAUTH_HEAD-PRIMOD = V_MODULEID.
9829
9830     IMPORT V_REMARKS_HEAD TO V_REMARKS_HEAD FROM MEMORY ID 'ID2'.
9831
9832
9833     ZAUTH_HEAD-REMARKS  = V_REMARKS_HEAD.
9834
9835     IMPORT ZUSERID TO ZUSERID FROM MEMORY ID 'ID'.
9836     ZAUTH_HEAD-REQUESTED_BY  = ZUSERID.
9837
9838     IMPORT ZAPPROVER TO ZAPPROVER FROM MEMORY ID 'ID1'.
9839     ZAUTH_HEAD-APPROVED_BY = ZAPPROVER.
9840
9841
9842     CLEAR UPL_TAB. REFRESH UPL_TAB.
9843     CLEAR UPL_TABX. REFRESH UPL_TABX.
9844
9845
9846
9847
9848     CLEAR:WA_ROLES1.
9849     LOOP AT IT_ROLES1 INTO WA_ROLES1.
9850       UPL_TABX-CPF_NO = WA_ROLES1-USERID.
9851       UPL_TABX-ROLE = WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
9852       UPL_TABX-FROM_DAT = WA_ROLES1-FR_DATE_AUTH.
9853       UPL_TABX-TO_DAT  = WA_ROLES1-TO_DATE_AUTH.
9854       APPEND  UPL_TABX.
9855     ENDLOOP.
9856
9857     LOOP AT UPL_TABX.
9858
9859       UPL_TAB-CPF_NO = UPL_TABX-CPF_NO.
9860       UPL_TAB-ROLE   = UPL_TABX-ROLE.
9861
9862   *******************************************************
9863       DATEO = UPL_TABX-FROM_DAT.
9864       CONCATENATE DATEO+6(4) DATEO+3(2) DATEO+0(2) INTO DATE.
9865   ***************************************************
9866       UPL_TAB-FROM_DAT   = DATE.
9867
9868       CLEAR : DATEO, DATE.
9869
9870       DATEO = UPL_TABX-TO_DAT.
9871       CONCATENATE DATEO+6(4) DATEO+3(2) DATEO+0(2) INTO DATE.
9872
9873       UPL_TAB-TO_DAT   = DATE.
9874
9875       CLEAR : DATEO, DATE.
9876
9877
9878   ***************************************************
9879
9880       APPEND UPL_TAB.
9881
9882     ENDLOOP.
9883   ***********************
9884     REFRESH UPL_TABX.
9885     CLEAR   UPL_TABX.
9886   ***********************
9887     IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
9888       XXCPF_NO = 'XX'.
9889       XXROLE   = 'XX'.
9890   *******************************************************
9891       LOOP AT UPL_TAB.
9892         TRANSLATE UPL_TAB TO UPPER CASE.
9893         MODIFY UPL_TAB.
9894       ENDLOOP.
9895   ***********************************************************************
9896       LOOP AT UPL_TAB.
9897         IF UPL_TAB-CPF_NO EQ SPACE AND
9898            UPL_TAB-ROLE EQ SPACE.
9899           DELETE UPL_TAB.
9900           CONTINUE.
9901         ENDIF.
9902         IF UPL_TAB-CPF_NO EQ SPACE.
9903           UPL_TAB-CPF_NO = XXCPF_NO.
9904         ENDIF.
9905         IF UPL_TAB-ROLE EQ SPACE.
9906           UPL_TAB-ROLE  = XXROLE.
9907         ENDIF.
9908
9909         IF UPL_TAB-FROM_DAT EQ '00000000' .
9910           UPL_TAB-FROM_DAT = SY-DATUM .
9911         ENDIF .
9912         IF UPL_TAB-TO_DAT EQ '00000000' .
9913           UPL_TAB-TO_DAT = '99991231' .
9914         ENDIF .
9915
9916
9917         MODIFY UPL_TAB.
9918         XXCPF_NO = UPL_TAB-CPF_NO.
9919         XXROLE   = UPL_TAB-ROLE.
9920       ENDLOOP.
9921       SORT UPL_TAB.
9922       DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM UPL_TAB.
9923
9924       LOOP AT UPL_TAB.
9925         XFLAG = 'N'.
9926         UPL_TABX-CPF_NO = UPL_TAB-CPF_NO.
9927         UPL_TABX-ROLE   = UPL_TAB-ROLE.
9928
9929         UPL_TABX-FROM_DAT = UPL_TAB-FROM_DAT.
9930         UPL_TABX-TO_DAT   = UPL_TAB-TO_DAT.
9931
9932
9933         SELECT SINGLE * FROM USR02 WHERE
9934              BNAME = UPL_TAB-CPF_NO.
9935         IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
9936           UPL_TABX-USER_NA = 'X'.
9937         ENDIF.
9938
9939         SELECT SINGLE * FROM AGR_DEFINE WHERE
9940            AGR_NAME = UPL_TAB-ROLE.
9941         IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
9942           UPL_TABX-ROLE_NA = 'X'.
9943         ENDIF.
9944
9945
9946         IF UPL_TABX-USER_NA = 'X'.
9947           DELETE UPL_TAB.
9948
9949           CLEAR UPL_TABX.
9950           CONTINUE.
9951         ELSE .
9952
9953           SELECT AGR_NAME UNAME FROM_DAT TO_DAT INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS
9954           OF TABLE L_AGR_USERS FROM AGR_USERS
9955           WHERE AGR_NAME = UPL_TABX-ROLE AND UNAME = UPL_TABX-CPF_NO .
9956
9957           IF SY-SUBRC  EQ 0 .
9958             SORT L_AGR_USERS BY TO_DAT ASCENDING .
9959             CLEAR L_AGR_USERS .
9960             LOOP AT L_AGR_USERS .
9961               IF L_AGR_USERS-FROM_DAT <= UPL_TABX-FROM_DAT AND
9962                  L_AGR_USERS-TO_DAT >= UPL_TABX-TO_DAT .
9963                 XFLAG = 'X'.
9964                 EXIT.
9965               ELSE .
9966                 IF L_AGR_USERS-FROM_DAT <= UPL_TABX-FROM_DAT AND
9967                    L_AGR_USERS-TO_DAT >= UPL_TABX-FROM_DAT .
9968                   UPL_TAB-FROM_DAT = L_AGR_USERS-TO_DAT .
9969                   MODIFY UPL_TAB .
9970                 ENDIF .
9971                 IF L_AGR_USERS-FROM_DAT <= UPL_TABX-TO_DAT AND
9972                    L_AGR_USERS-TO_DAT >= UPL_TABX-TO_DAT .
9973                   UPL_TAB-TO_DAT = L_AGR_USERS-FROM_DAT .
9974                   MODIFY UPL_TAB .
9975                 ENDIF .
9976               ENDIF .
9977             ENDLOOP .
9978           ENDIF .
9979   **
9980           CLEAR UPL_TABX.
9981         ENDIF.
9982         IF XFLAG = 'X'.
9983           DELETE UPL_TAB.
9984         ENDIF.
9985         CLEAR UPL_TABX.
9986
9987       ENDLOOP.
9988     ENDIF.
9989     LOOP AT UPL_TAB.
9990       MOVE-CORRESPONDING UPL_TAB TO G_ROLE_ITAB.
9991       G_ROLE_ITAB-ITEM_NO = ZITEM_NO.
9992       APPEND G_ROLE_ITAB.
9993       ZITEM_NO = ZITEM_NO + 1.
9994     ENDLOOP.
9995
9996
9997   ***************************************************************
9998   ***************************************************************
9999
10000    """""""""""""""""""""""""""
10001    """"""""for text
10002    LOOP AT G_ROLE_ITAB.
10003
10004      SELECT SINGLE * FROM USR21 WHERE BNAME = G_ROLE_ITAB-CPF_NO.
10005      SELECT NAME_TEXT INTO G_ROLE_ITAB-USER_NAME
10006   FROM ADRP UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE PERSNUMBER = USR21-PERSNUMBER
10007   ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
10008   ENDSELECT.
10009
10010      SELECT TEXT INTO G_ROLE_ITAB-TEXT
10011   FROM AGR_TEXTS UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE AGR_NAME = G_ROLE_ITAB-ROLE AND SPRAS = 'EN'
10012   ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
10013   ENDSELECT.
10014      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
10015      ELSE.
10016        DELETE G_ROLE_ITAB WHERE ROLE = G_ROLE_ITAB-ROLE.
10017      ENDIF.
10018      MODIFY G_ROLE_ITAB.
10019    ENDLOOP.
10020    """""""""""""""""""""""""""""
10021    """"""""""""""""""""""""
10022
10023    """"""""""""""""
10024
10025
10026    IF  G_ROLE_ITAB[]  IS  NOT INITIAL.
10027  ***********************************************************************
10028      IF ZAUTH_HEAD-AUTH_REQ_NO IS INITIAL.
10029
10030
10031        PERFORM GET_NEXT_NUMBER_ASN.
10032
10033        ZAUTH_HEAD-AUTH_REQ_NO = ZGET_NUMBER.
10034
10035      ENDIF.
10036      MODIFY ZAUTH_HEAD.
10037
10038
10039      SET PARAMETER ID 'ZROLEREQNO' FIELD ZGET_NUMBER.
10040
10041  ******************************************************************
10042
10043      ZAUTH_ITEM-AUTH_REQ_NO = ZAUTH_HEAD-AUTH_REQ_NO.
10044      ZITEM_NO = 1.
10045      DELETE FROM ZAUTH_ITEM
10046         WHERE AUTH_REQ_NO = ZAUTH_HEAD-AUTH_REQ_NO.
10047
10048  ***************************Added by Abhishek - Delimit existing roles.
10049      IF IT_AGR IS NOT INITIAL.
10050
10051        CLEAR S_ITAB.
10052        LOOP AT G_ROLE_ITAB.
10053          MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_ROLE_ITAB TO S_ITAB.
10054        ENDLOOP.
10055
10056        LOOP AT IT_AGR INTO WA_AGR WHERE AGR_NAME NE 'M:COMMON_USER_TOOLS'.
10057          S_ITAB-ITEM_NO = S_ITAB-ITEM_NO + 1.
10058          G_ROLE_ITAB-ITEM_NO = S_ITAB-ITEM_NO.
10059          G_ROLE_ITAB-CPF_NO = S_ITAB-CPF_NO.
10060          G_ROLE_ITAB-ROLE = WA_AGR-AGR_NAME.
10061          G_ROLE_ITAB-TEXT = WA_AGR-AGR_TEXT.
10062          G_ROLE_ITAB-USER_NAME = S_ITAB-USER_NAME.
10063          G_ROLE_ITAB-FROM_DAT = WA_AGR-FROM_DAT.
10064          G_ROLE_ITAB-TO_DAT = SY-DATUM.
10065          APPEND G_ROLE_ITAB.
10066        ENDLOOP.
10067
10068      ENDIF.
10069  ****************************************************End of addition by Abhishek
10070
10071      LOOP AT G_ROLE_ITAB.
10072        IF G_ROLE_ITAB-CPF_NO NE SPACE AND
10073           G_ROLE_ITAB-ROLE NE SPACE .
10074
10075          ZAUTH_ITEM-FROM_DAT = G_ROLE_ITAB-FROM_DAT .
10076          ZAUTH_ITEM-TO_DAT = G_ROLE_ITAB-TO_DAT .
10077  ************************************************************************
10078          ZAUTH_ITEM-CPF_NO = G_ROLE_ITAB-CPF_NO.
10079          ZAUTH_ITEM-ROLE = G_ROLE_ITAB-ROLE.
10080          ZAUTH_ITEM-ITEM_NO = ZITEM_NO.
10081          ZITEM_NO = ZITEM_NO + 1.
10082          MODIFY ZAUTH_ITEM.
10083        ENDIF.
10084      ENDLOOP.
10085      ZAUTH_EXCP-AUTH_REQ_NO = ZAUTH_HEAD-AUTH_REQ_NO.
10086
10087      LOOP AT UPL_TABX.
10088        ZAUTH_EXCP-CPF_NO = UPL_TABX-CPF_NO.
10089        ZAUTH_EXCP-ROLE   = UPL_TABX-ROLE.
10090        ZAUTH_EXCP-REMARKS = UPL_TABX-REMARKS.
10091        ZAUTH_EXCP-ROLE_NA = UPL_TABX-ROLE_NA.
10092        ZAUTH_EXCP-USER_NA = UPL_TABX-USER_NA.
10093        MODIFY ZAUTH_EXCP.
10094      ENDLOOP.
10095
10096    ELSE.
10097      V_MESSAGE_AS = 'X'.
10098    ENDIF.

10100  ENDFORM.                    " ROLE_HELP

10109  FORM GET_NEXT_NUMBER_ASN .

10112      EXPORTING
10113        NR_RANGE_NR = '01'
10114        OBJECT      = 'ZROLEREQ'
10115        QUANTITY    = '1'
10116  *     SUBOBJECT   = ' '
10117  *     TOYEAR      = '0000'
10118  *     IGNORE_BUFFER                 = ' '
10119      IMPORTING
10120        NUMBER      = ZGET_NUMBER
10121  *     QUANTITY    =
10122        RETURNCODE  = RC
10123  * EXCEPTIONS
10124  *     INTERVAL_NOT_FOUND            = 1
10125  *     NUMBER_RANGE_NOT_INTERN       = 2
10126  *     OBJECT_NOT_FOUND              = 3
10127  *     QUANTITY_IS_0                 = 4
10128  *     QUANTITY_IS_NOT_1             = 5
10129  *     INTERVAL_OVERFLOW             = 6
10130  *     BUFFER_OVERFLOW               = 7
10131  *     OTHERS      = 8
10132      .
10133    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
10134  * MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
10135  *         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
10136    ENDIF.

10146  FORM INSERT_ITEMS_OLM_ASSIGN .
10147    DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
10148    CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB.
10149
10150    SORT G_TC_117_ITAB
10151    BY ROLE_NAME.
10152
10153    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TC_117_ITAB
10154      COMPARING ROLE_NAME.
10155
10156    LOOP AT G_TC_117_ITAB INTO G_TC_117_WA .
10157
10158      MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TC_117_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
10159
10160      IF G_ROLE_FLAG = 'X' AND WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL = '' AND
10161           WA_ITEMTAB-STATUS = '' AND WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_REQUEST = ''.
10162        WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_REQUEST = ZROLEREQNO.
10163      ENDIF.
10164
10165      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE'.
10166        WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
10167      ENDIF.
10168
10169      WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
10170      IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
10171        WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
10172      ENDIF.
10173      IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
10174        I = I + 1.
10175        WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
10176        APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
10177      ENDIF.
10178
10179      G_I = I.
10180
10181  *    PERFORM check_module_wise.
10182
10183    ENDLOOP.
10184
10185    DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
10186
10187    IF G_LINES_RL = 0.
10188      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
10189        IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
10190          SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
10191          MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
10192        ENDIF.
10193      ELSE.
10194        ROLLBACK WORK.
10195      ENDIF.
10196    ELSE.
10197
10198      DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO
10199          AND MODULEID = MODULEID.
10200
10201      MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
10202
10203      IF SY-SUBRC = 0 AND G_ROLE_FLAG <> 'X'.
10204        MESSAGE I045(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
10205      ENDIF.
10206
10207    ENDIF.
10208  ENDFORM.                    " INSERT_ITEMS_OLM_ASSIGN

10249  *&      Form  INSERT_ITEMS_SRM

10251  *       text
10252  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
10253  *  -->  p1        text
10254  *  <--  p2        text
10255  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
10256  FORM INSERT_ITEMS_SRM .
10257    DATA : I LIKE SY-INDEX .
10258    CLEAR : WA_ITEMTAB, IST_ITEMTAB.
10259
10260    SORT G_TABLCTRL118_ITAB
10261    BY ROLE_NAME PLANT GRP  SLOC RECEIPT_LOC APPROVER.
10262
10263    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM G_TABLCTRL118_ITAB
10264      COMPARING ROLE_NAME PLANT GRP  SLOC RECEIPT_LOC APPROVER REJ_FL
10265      ROLE_TYPE_EX CRC_POS.
10266
10267    LOOP AT G_TABLCTRL118_ITAB INTO G_TABLCTRL118_WA.
10268
10269      MOVE-CORRESPONDING G_TABLCTRL118_WA TO WA_ITEMTAB.
10270
10271      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR
10272         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' OR
10273         OLD_OK_CODE = 'CRCROLES'.
10274        WA_ITEMTAB-DOCNO = ZDOCNUMB.
10275      ENDIF.
10276
10277      WA_ITEMTAB-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
10278      IF WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL <> ''.
10279        WA_ITEMTAB-REJ_FL_SAVE = 'X'.
10280      ENDIF.
10281      IF NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
10282        I = I + 1.
10283        WA_ITEMTAB-SRNO = I .
10284        APPEND WA_ITEMTAB TO IST_ITEMTAB.
10285      ENDIF.
10286
10287      G_I = I.
10288
10289      SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZSR_PREP_ROLEDES WHERE ROLE_TYPE =
10290                                                       WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME.
10291      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
10292
10293
10294
10295        IF ZSR_PREP_ROLEDES-P_GRP = 'X' AND
10296                       ( OLD_OK_CODE = 'APPROVE' OR
10297                      OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' OR
10298                      OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'  OR
10299                      OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE'  OR
10300                      OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' ) AND
10301                      NOT WA_ITEMTAB-ROLE_NAME IS INITIAL.
10302
10303          IF WA_ITEMTAB-GRP IS INITIAL.
10304            G_FIELD = 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI-GRP'.
10305            ROLLBACK WORK.
10306            MESSAGE I085(ZHELP) WITH G_I.
10307            CLEAR OKCODE_100.
10308            CALL SCREEN 100.
10309          ENDIF.
10310        ENDIF.
10311      ENDIF.
10312
10313      CLEAR:COUNT_GRP,G_WA_PGRP.
10314
10315      LOOP AT G_TABLCTRL118_ITAB INTO G_WA_PGRP WHERE  GRP = WA_ITEMTAB-GRP  .
10316        IF G_WA_PGRP-GRP  IS NOT INITIAL.
10317          COUNT_GRP = COUNT_GRP + 1.
10318        ENDIF.
10319      ENDLOOP.
10320      IF  COUNT_GRP > '1'.
10321        MESSAGE I092(ZHELP) .
10322        CLEAR OKCODE_100.
10323        CALL SCREEN 100.
10324      ENDIF.
10325
10326    ENDLOOP.
10327
10328    DESCRIBE TABLE IST_ITEMTAB LINES G_LINES_RL.
10329
10330  ***added g_reset_fl to check resetting & no rollback
10331    IF G_LINES_RL = 0 .
10332      ROLLBACK WORK.
10333      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CHANGE'.
10334
10335        IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
10336          SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO'.
10337          MESSAGE I099(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
10338        ENDIF.
10339      ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO' .
10340        MESSAGE I103(ZHELP) WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
10341      ELSE.
10342        ROLLBACK WORK.
10343      ENDIF.
10344    ELSE.
10345      IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'RELEASE' AND G_LINES_RL = 0.
10346        ROLLBACK WORK.
10347        MESSAGE I089(ZHELP).
10348      ELSE.
10349
10350        IF OLD_OK_CODE = 'CREATE' OR OLD_OK_CODE = 'CROSSCO'
10351  .
10352        ELSEIF OLD_OK_CODE <> 'DISPLAY'.
10353          DELETE FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI WHERE
10354          DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO AND
10355          MODULEID = MODULEID..
10356        ENDIF.
10357
10358        MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREI FROM TABLE IST_ITEMTAB.
10359
10360
10361      ENDIF.
10362
10363    ENDIF.
10364  ENDFORM.                    " INSERT_ITEMS_SRM

10366  *&      Form  CREATE_ROLES_SRM

10373  FORM CREATE_ROLES_SRM .
10374    CLEAR:L_LOGSYS.
10375
10376
10377
10378    SELECT SINGLE LOGSYS FROM ZMM_LOGSYS INTO L_LOGSYS
10379    WHERE  APPL = 'SRM'.
10380
10381    """"""calling srm
10382
10383    IF NOT L_LOGSYS  IS INITIAL.
10384
10385      LOOP AT   G_TABLCTRL118_ITAB INTO G_TABLCTRL118_WA.
10386
10387        WA_ROLES_SRMP-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
10388        WA_ROLES_SRMP-ROLE_NAME = G_TABLCTRL118_WA-ROLE_NAME.
10389        WA_ROLES_SRMP-CCODE = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE.
10390        WA_ROLES_SRMP-FROM_DAT = SY-DATUM.
10391        WA_ROLES_SRMP-TO_DAT   = '99991231'.
10392        WA_ROLES_SRMP-GRP =  G_TABLCTRL118_WA-GRP.
10393        APPEND  WA_ROLES_SRMP TO IT_ROLES_SRMP.
10394
10395      ENDLOOP.
10396
10397
10398
10399
10400      P_UNAME = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
10401
10402      SELECT SINGLE * FROM ZBCUSRMST  INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF WA_ZBCUSRMST
10403        WHERE CPFNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
10404
10405      P_FNAME        = WA_ZBCUSRMST-FIRST_NAME.
10406      P_LNAME        = WA_ZBCUSRMST-LAST_NAME.
10407      P_CCODE =    ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE.
10408
10409
10410
10411
10412
10413
10414      CALL FUNCTION 'ZSRM_ROLE_ASSIGN_ARMS' DESTINATION L_LOGSYS
10415        EXPORTING
10416          P_UNAME       = P_UNAME
10417          P_FNAME       = P_FNAME
10418          P_LNAME       = P_LNAME
10419          P_CCODE       = P_CCODE
10420        TABLES
10421          IT_ROLES_SRMP = IT_ROLES_SRMP
10422          ITAB_RETURN   = ITAB_RETURN.
10423
10424      IF ITAB_RETURN[] IS NOT INITIAL.
10425
10426        V_SRM_ST = 'C'.
10427
10428        LOOP AT ITAB_RETURN INTO WA_RETURN.
10429
10430          IF   WA_RETURN-STATUS NE  'C'.
10431            V_SRM_ST = 'IF'.
10432          ELSE.
10433
10434          ENDIF.
10435        ENDLOOP.
10436
10437        IF  V_SRM_ST = 'C'.
10438          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'C'.
10439
10440        ELSE.
10441          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IF'.
10442          PERFORM SEND_SAPMAIL_SRMASSIGN .
10443        ENDIF.
10444
10445
10446        V_ROLEREQ-DOCNO = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO.
10447        P_UNAME_SMS = P_UNAME.
10448        G_USERID_N = ''.
10449        MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ.
10450        IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
10451          IF  ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'C'.
10452
10453            CALL FUNCTION 'ZMM_SEND_SMS'
10454              EXPORTING
10455                CPFNO_S     = G_USERID_N
10456                CPFNO_R     = P_UNAME_SMS
10457                FROM_DAT    = SY-DATUM
10458                TO_DAT      = '99991231'
10459                AUTH_REQ_NO = V_ROLEREQ-DOCNO
10460              IMPORTING
10461                FLAG_MSG    = L_FLAG_MSG.
10462
10463            PERFORM SEND_SAPMAIL_SRMASSIGN .
10464
10465          ENDIF.
10466        ENDIF.
10467
10468
10469      ELSE.
10470        IF  V_SRM_ST = ''.
10471          ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'N'.
10472          MODIFY ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ FROM ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ.
10473        ENDIF.
10474
10475      ENDIF.
10476
10477      PERFORM UNLOCK_RECORD.
10478
10479      CLEAR:V_MESSAGE_SRM.
10480      IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'C'.
10481
10482        CONCATENATE 'Roles assigned for request No .' ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO INTO
10483        V_MESSAGE_SRM SEPARATED BY SPACE.
10484
10485        MESSAGE I735(ZMM) WITH V_MESSAGE_SRM.
10486
10487      ELSE.
10488
10489        CONCATENATE 'Roles not  assigned for request No .' ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO INTO
10490     V_MESSAGE_SRM SEPARATED BY SPACE.
10491
10492        MESSAGE I735(ZMM) WITH V_MESSAGE_SRM.
10493      ENDIF.
10494
10495      LEAVE PROGRAM.
10496    ENDIF.
10497
10498
10499  ENDFORM.                    " CREATE_ROLES_SRM
10500  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
10501  *&      Form  SEND_SAPMAIL_SRMASSIGN
10502  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
10503  *       text
10504  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
10505  *  -->  p1        text
10506  *  <--  p2        text
10507  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
10508  FORM SEND_SAPMAIL_SRMASSIGN .
10509    DOCUMENT_DATA-OBJ_LANGU  = SY-LANGU.
10510    DOCUMENT_DATA-OBJ_NAME   = 'ICE Core Team'.
10511    DOCUMENT_DATA-OBJ_DESCR  = 'Mail from ICE Core Team'.
10512
10513    CONCATENATE DOCUMENT_DATA-OBJ_DESCR '---' MODULEID
10514    '-' 'Module' INTO DOCUMENT_DATA-OBJ_DESCR.
10515    DOCUMENT_DATA-PRIORITY   = '3'.
10516
10517  * Remove prefix 'US' from receiver
10518    REFRESH RECEIVERS.
10519
10520    CLEAR WA_RECEIVERS.
10521    WA_RECEIVERS-RECEIVER = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDCR.
10522    WA_RECEIVERS-REC_TYPE = 'B'.
10523    WA_RECEIVERS-EXPRESS  = 'X'.
10524    APPEND WA_RECEIVERS TO RECEIVERS.
10525
10526    CLEAR WA_RECEIVERS.
10527
10528    MOVE SPACE TO OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE.
10529    APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10530
10531    CONCATENATE  'Subject: '  'Creation of Roles for userid '
10532  ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID INTO  OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE
10533  SEPARATED BY SPACE.
10534    APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10535
10536    MOVE SPACE TO OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE.
10537    APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10538    IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'C'.
10539
10540
10541      CONCATENATE 'Please  check  your role request  which  has'
10542       'been assigned  &  completed - ' ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO INTO
10543  OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE
10544  SEPARATED BY SPACE.
10545      APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10546    ELSE.
10547
10548    ENDIF.
10549  ********************************************************************
10550    """"""""""""""""""""""
10551    IF ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'IF'.
10552
10553      CONCATENATE ' Roles are not assigned for Request no.- ' ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-DOCNO INTO
10554  OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE
10555  SEPARATED BY SPACE.
10556
10557      APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10558    ENDIF.
10559    """""""""""""""""""""""""""""
10560  ********************************************************************
10561    MOVE SPACE TO OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE.
10562    APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10563
10564    OBJECT_CONTENT-LINE = 'ICE Core Team'.
10565    APPEND OBJECT_CONTENT.
10566
10567    CALL FUNCTION 'SO_NEW_DOCUMENT_SEND_API1'
10568      EXPORTING
10569        DOCUMENT_DATA              = DOCUMENT_DATA
10570        DOCUMENT_TYPE              = 'RAW'
10571        PUT_IN_OUTBOX              = 'X'
10572      IMPORTING
10573        SENT_TO_ALL                = SENT_TO_ALL
10574      TABLES
10575        OBJECT_HEADER              = OBJHEAD
10576        OBJECT_CONTENT             = OBJECT_CONTENT
10577        RECEIVERS                  = RECEIVERS
10578      EXCEPTIONS
10579        TOO_MANY_RECEIVERS         = 01
10580        DOCUMENT_NOT_SENT          = 02
10581        DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_EXIST    = 03
10582        OPERATION_NO_AUTHORIZATION = 04
10583        PARAMETER_ERROR            = 05
10584        X_ERROR                    = 06
10585        ENQUEUE_ERROR              = 07.
10586
10587    CASE SY-SUBRC.
10588      WHEN 0.
10589
10590  *      MESSAGE i060(zhelp) WITH zic_prep_rolereq-useridcr.
10591      WHEN '01'.
10592        RAISE TOO_MANY_RECEIVERS.
10593      WHEN '02'.
10594        RAISE DOCUMENT_NOT_SENT.
10595      WHEN '03'.
10596        RAISE DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_EXIST.
10597      WHEN '04'.
10598        RAISE OPERATION_NO_AUTHORIZATION.
10599      WHEN '05'.
10600        RAISE PARAMETER_ERROR.
10601      WHEN '06'.
10602        RAISE X_ERROR.
10603      WHEN '07'.
10604        RAISE ENQUEUE_ERROR.
10605    ENDCASE.
10606
10607  ENDFORM.                    " SEND_SAPMAIL_SRMASSIGN

11038    SEARCH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME FOR 'CCC'.
11039    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
11040      FLAG = 'X'.
11041      WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
11042      WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME.
11043      REPLACE 'CCC' WITH ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE+0(3) INTO
11044                                 WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
11045      APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
11046    ENDIF.
11047
11048    SEARCH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME FOR 'AAA'.
11049    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
11050      FLAG = 'X'.
11051      WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
11052      WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME.
11053      REPLACE 'AAA' WITH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ASSET INTO
11054                                  WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
11055      APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
11056    ENDIF.
11057
11058    SEARCH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME FOR 'BBB'.
11059    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
11060      FLAG = 'X'.
11061      WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
11062      WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME.
11063      REPLACE 'BBB' WITH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-BASIN INTO
11064                                  WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
11065      APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
11066    ENDIF.
11067
11068    SEARCH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME FOR 'XXYY'.
11069    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
11070      FLAG = 'X'.
11071      WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
11072      WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME.
11073      REPLACE 'XX' WITH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-PROJECT INTO
11074                                  WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
11075      REPLACE 'YY' WITH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-LOCATION INTO
11076                                  WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
11077      APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
11078    ENDIF.
11079
11080    SEARCH WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME FOR 'ZZZ'.
11081    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
11082      FLAG = 'X'.
11083      WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
11084      WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME.
11085      REPLACE 'ZZZ' WITH 'ALL' INTO
11086                                  WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME.
11087      APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
11088    ENDIF.
11089
11090    IF FLAG <> 'X'.
11091      WA_ROLES1-USERID = ZIC_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERID.
11092      WA_ROLES1-ROLE_NAME = WA_ITEMTAB_SL-ROLE_NAME.
11093      APPEND WA_ROLES1 TO IT_ROLES1.
11094    ENDIF.
11095
11096    CLEAR FLAG.
11097
11098  ENDFORM.                    " insert_data_ps

11113      EXPORTING

11116      TABLES
11117  *     PARAMETER      =
11118  *     PROFILES       =
11119        ACTIVITYGROUPS = IT_AGR
11120        RETURN         = IT_RETN.
11121
11122  *  IF tmp_agr IS NOT INITIAL.
11123  *    LOOP AT tmp_agr INTO wa_agr WHERE agr_name NE 'M:COMMON_USER_TOOLS'.
11124  *      wa_agr-to_dat = sy-datum.
11125  *      APPEND wa_agr TO it_agr.

11127  *  ENDIF.
